### [1] [Occupancy Driven Phase Transitions in Gapped Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31564)
*Ian Madden,Alex Kociubinski,Wouter Montfrooij*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 能隙系统在升温时会激发越来越多的热激发。作者指出，当激发数达到临界值时，激发种群变得热力学不稳定，系统在两个状态间发生转变；此转变以比热发散为标志，但不需要序参量，也不要求能隙完全闭合。论文以二能级系统、超流⁴He举例，并论证URu₂Si₂中的隐序（hidden order）转变正是这种无序参量、由占据数驱动的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释一类无需序参量、也不伴随能隙完全闭合的相变：即由热激发占据数达到临界值所驱动的热力学不稳定性转变，并试图为URu₂Si₂隐序转变提供新的物理图像。

Method: 将激发间的相互作用类比于受阻尼影响的谐振子（频率随占据数改变），考察激发种群随温度增长直至临界值时的热力学稳定性；当占据数达到临界值，系统在两种状态间发生转变，具体表征为比热发散。通过二能级系统和超流⁴He两个例子说明该热力学机制。

Result: 一旦激发数达到临界值，激发种群在热力学上变得不稳定，系统发生状态转变；该转变以比热发散为标志，且无需序参量、无需能隙完全闭合。URu₂Si₂的隐序转变可被理解为这种由占据数驱动、无序参量的转变。

Conclusion: 存在一类由热激发占据数达到临界值驱动的热力学转变，其标志是比热发散，既不需要序参量也不要求能隙闭合；URu₂Si₂的隐序转变即为该类机制的一个实例。

Abstract: Systems characterized by an energy gap between the ground state and excited states support a growing number of thermal excitations upon raising the temperature. Provided these excitations interact with each other, their number affects the size of the energy gap akin to a harmonic oscillator where the oscillation frequency is damping dependent. Here we show that once a critical number of excitations is reached, the excitation population becomes thermodynamically unstable and the system transitions between two states. This transition is marked by a divergence in the specific heat, which does not have to be accompanied by an order parameter, nor by the full closure of the gap. We illustrate the thermodynamics using a simple two level system and superfluid $^4$He as examples, and we argue that the hidden order transition in URu$_2$Si$_2$ is an example of such an occupancy driven transition in the absence of an order parameter.

</details>

### [2] [Discrete time liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30266)
*Zuo Wang,Paul M. Schindler,Marin Bukov,Marios H. Michael*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了"离散时间液体"这一新型时空序：在有限温度光腔驱动下，二维自旋系统虽具有稳健的倍周期动力学，但时间相干性有限，个体轨迹会在两种时间构型间随机切换，这种切换被识别为时间孤子（时间序中的拓扑缺陷），从而破坏长程时间相干，形成时间液体态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自离散时间晶体的概念出现以来，一个核心问题是：是否还存在其他类型的时间多体序。作者旨在探索一种区别于时间晶体（长程时间相干、破缺离散时间平移对称性）的新时空序，即时间相干性有限、但局部仍保持倍周期序的物态。

Method: 作者研究一个与有限温度光腔耦合的二维自旋系统，利用光腔涨落提供噪扰的、近似周期但时间相干性有限的驱动。在该系统中观察到稳健的倍周期（周期倍增）动力学亚稳态。作者将个体轨迹在两种对称相关时间构型之间的随机切换识别为时间孤子（时间序中的拓扑缺陷），并提出一个能重现多体系统时间关联分布的最小模型，把时间液体描述为含有限时间孤子密度的时间晶体。

Result: 在扩展参数范围内，自旋系统形成周期倍增响应的亚稳态；与时间晶体不同，该态仅有有限的时间相干性，个体轨迹在两种时间构型间随机切换。这些切换对应时间孤子，它们保持局部倍周期同时破坏长程时间相干。最小模型成功重现了多体系统的时间关联分布。由于时间孤子可在任意时刻出现，系综平均恢复了连续时间平移对称性。

Conclusion: 离散时间液体是一种由时间孤子产生、具有短程时间相干的亚稳态时空序，可被理解为含有限时间孤子密度的时间晶体。这一发现提出了离散时间液体可能存在于热平衡中的有趣可能性。

Abstract: Discrete time crystals exhibit robust subharmonic oscillations, spontaneously breaking the discrete time-translation symmetry of a periodic drive. Since their emergence, the question of whether other types of temporal many-body order can exist has attracted considerable attention. Here we identify discrete time liquids -- a new form of spatiotemporal order characterized by short-range temporal coherence as a result of time-solitons. We study a two-dimensional spin system coupled to a finite-temperature cavity, whose fluctuations provide a noisy, approximately periodic drive with only finite temporal coherence. Remarkably, the spins nevertheless form a metastable state with robust period-doubled dynamics over an extended parameter regime. Unlike a time crystal, this state has only finite temporal coherence: individual trajectories stochastically switch between the two symmetry-related temporal configurations of the period-doubled response. We identify these switches as time-solitons: topological defects in temporal order that preserve local period doubling while destroying long-range temporal coherence, thereby producing the time liquid state. We propose a minimal model which reproduces the distribution of temporal correlations of the many-body system, suggesting a general description of the time liquid as a time crystal with a finite density of time-solitons. Because these solitons can occur at arbitrary times, ensemble averaging restores continuous time-translation symmetry, raising the intriguing possibility that discrete time liquids can exist in thermal equilibrium.

</details>


### [3] [Charge Density Waves and In-Gap States in Coupled Chains and Moiré Superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30281)
*Muhammad Akram,Onur Erten*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究一维耦合Su-Schrieffer-Heeger链中的电荷密度波（CDW）相及其对称性保护的束缚态，涵盖晶格匹配的梯子结构和晶格失配的莫尔超晶格，发现链间隧穿与晶格失配是调控CDW序和束缚态的控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷密度波源于电子与晶格畸变的相互作用，是低维体系的标志性现象。作者旨在系统研究一维耦合链中电子-声子耦合与链间隧穿、晶格失配之间的竞争如何产生丰富的CDW相及束缚态，以揭示多链和莫尔结构下的新物理。

Method: 以耦合Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）链为模型，分别研究晶格匹配的梯子（ladder）与晶格失配的莫尔超晶格两类结构；分析电子-声子耦合强度与链间隧穿之间的竞争，考察由此产生的CDW相序，并结合晶格对称性分析束缚态的成因与能级位置。

Result: 在晶格匹配链中，发现交错二聚体、三聚体及非公度态等一系列CDW相，这些相通常有能隙，其束缚态由晶体对称性（而非手性对称性）保护，因此不钉扎在零能。晶格失配进一步将这些相重构为长周期的超莫尔CDW，并伴随钉扎在莫尔尺度上的有序缺陷阵列，缺陷处局域化电子态。链间隧穿与晶格失配由此成为调控CDW序和束缚态的自然控制参数。

Conclusion: 一维耦合SSH链中，链间隧穿与晶格失配可驱动丰富的CDW相并产生对称性保护的束缚态；晶格失配引入的长周期超莫尔CDW及其缺陷局域态表明莫尔工程是调控一维电子序的有效途径。

Abstract: Charge density waves (CDWs) arise from the interplay between electrons and lattice distortions and are a hallmark of low-dimensional systems. Here we study coupled Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chains, both lattice-matched ladders and lattice-mismatched moiré superlattices, and find a rich set of CDW phases together with symmetry-protected bound states. In lattice-matched chains, the competition between electron-phonon coupling and interchain tunneling drives a sequence of CDW phases, including staggered dimers, trimers, and incommensurate states. These phases are generally gapped and host bound states protected by crystalline symmetries rather than chiral symmetry, and are therefore not pinned to zero energy. Lattice mismatch further reconstructs these phases into long-period supermoiré CDWs, with an ordered array of defects, pinned to the moiré length scale, that host localized electronic states. Interchain tunneling and lattice mismatch thus emerge as natural control parameters for CDW order and bound states in one-dimensional chains.

</details>


### [4] [Nonlinear Canonical Transformations for Studying Spin-1/2 Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30403)
*Swarnamoy Ghosh,Thomas M. Henderson,Gustavo E. Scuseria*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非线性自旋-费米子-自旋（SFS）变换，将强关联自旋哈密顿量映射为平均场层面更容易描述的新自旋哈密顿量，并识别出将变换后乘积态映射为原表象中纠缠态的紧凑 Clifford 线路。在 XXZ 与 J1-J2 模型上的基准测试表明，该变换可显著改善自旋-BCS 平均场及耦合簇方法的精度，可作为强关联自旋系统的"多体预条件器"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联量子系统（如阻挫自旋格点）的基态常具有强多参考特征和对称性破缺，使传统耦合簇方法面临挑战；多参考与对称投影耦合簇理论尚非黑箱方法，需针对具体系统开发。自旋-BCS 耦合簇在高关联区可能需要高阶激发才能恢复精确基态物理。

Method: 引入非线性自旋-费米子-自旋（SFS）变换，将强关联自旋哈密顿量映射为在平均场层面描述更精确的新自旋哈密顿量；针对代表性 SFS 变换，识别出将变换后乘积态映射为原表象中经线路"装扮"、通常纠缠态的紧凑 Clifford 线路；在变换后表象中对哈密顿量采用破缺对称性平均场理论及后平均场方法（如自旋-BCS 耦合簇）处理。

Result: 在 XXZ 和 J1-J2 哈密顿量上的基准显示，变换后表象中的自旋平均场理论相比传统自旋-BCS 平均场显著减小能量误差；对一维 XXZ 模型，基于自旋-BCS 的耦合簇计算相对未变换方法精度大幅提升；在二维情形下，变换后平均场解的精度依赖于格点排序和边界条件（因可能出现非局域弦）。

Conclusion: 算符表示的精确变换可作为平均场和低秩耦合簇近似的"多体预条件器"，为强关联自旋系统的求解提供通用且有效的改进途径。

Abstract: Strongly correlated quantum systems, including frustrated spin lattices, remain challenging for conventional coupled-cluster methods because their ground states often exhibit strong multireference character and symmetry breaking. Although multireference and symmetry-projected coupled-cluster theories are promising, they are not yet black-box methods and require system-specific development. The XXZ and $J_1$-$J_2$ spin models provide demanding benchmarks. Spin-BCS-based coupled-cluster methods may require high excitation ranks to recover the exact ground-state physics in strongly correlated regimes. We introduce a nonlinear spin-fermion-spin (SFS) transformation designed to map a strongly correlated spin Hamiltonian into a new spin Hamiltonian whose ground state is more accurately described at the mean-field level. For a representative SFS transformation, we identify a compact Clifford circuit that maps transformed product states to circuit-dressed, generally entangled states in the original representation. The transformed Hamiltonian can then be treated using broken-symmetry mean-field theory and post-mean-field methods. Benchmarks for the XXZ and $J_1$-$J_2$ Hamiltonians show that spin mean-field theory in the transformed representation reduces the energy error relative to conventional spin-BCS mean field. The spin-BCS-based coupled-cluster calculations for the one-dimensional XXZ model provide substantially improved accuracy relative to the corresponding untransformed methods. In 2D, the accuracy of the transformed mean-field solutions depends on the site ordering and boundary conditions, as nonlocal strings can appear. These results show that exact transformations of operator representations can serve as many-body preconditioners for mean-field and low-rank coupled-cluster approximations to strongly correlated spin systems.

</details>


### [5] [$π$-Flux Tuning of Quantum Paramagnetism in the Triangular-Lattice Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30290)
*Zhaochen Liu,Andreas Feuerpfeil,Ronny Thomale*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 比较半填充下两种时间反演对称的三角晶格 Hubbard 模型（零通量与交错 π 通量），发现其环形交换项符号不同，导致一个倾向于手性自旋液体、另一个缺乏手性序，可利用通量调控的环形交换区分手性/非手性量子顺磁体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两种模型在强耦合极限下都退化为相同的海森堡模型，但它们的电荷涨落谱不同；作者希望解释为何二者在中强耦合下进入不同的量子顺磁（自旋液体）相，特别是手性起因的差异。

Method: 结合强耦合展开与无限密度矩阵重整化群（iDMRG）数值计算，比较两种模型的环形交换项符号及手性关联/标量手性（scalar chirality）行为。

Result: 零通量模型的环形交换为正，促进自发手性，与其中间耦合下的手性自旋液体图像一致；交错 π 通量模型（非相互作用极限为 Dirac 半金属）的环形交换变号，不利于手性序，且未观测到可分辨的标量手性。

Conclusion: 通量调控的环形交换是区分有限耦合下手性与非手性量子顺磁体的高效判据。

Abstract: We compare two time-reversal-symmetric triangular-lattice Hubbard models at half filling: the zero-flux model and a staggered $π$-flux model with zero and $π$ flux through oppositely oriented triangles. Although both reduce to the same Heisenberg model at leading order in the strong-coupling limit, their distinct charge fluctuation profiles yield different quantum paramagnetic regimes. Combining a strong-coupling expansion with infinite density matrix renormalization group (iDMRG) calculations, we find that the positive ring-exchange of the zero-flux model promotes spontaneous chirality, consistent with its notion of a chiral spin liquid at intermediate coupling. In the staggered-$π$-flux model, whose noninteracting limit is a Dirac semimetal, the ring exchange changes sign and disfavors chiral order and we find no resolvable scalar chirality. This renders flux-tuned ring-exchange an efficient discriminator between chiral and non-chiral quantum paramagnets at finite coupling.

</details>


### [6] [Extreme competition between stripes and superconductivity in the hole-doped Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30321)
*Ao Chen,Johnnie Gray,Si-Jing Du,Garnet Kin-Lic Chan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用神经张量网络和神经回流态模拟，在高达1024个格点的二维Hubbard模型上达到了前所未有的变分精度，发现在1/8掺杂下均匀d波态、超导条纹态和非超导条纹态之间存在毫t量级的近简并，表明体系处于条纹与超导的激烈竞争之中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维Hubbard模型最初被提出作为铜氧化物高温超导体中均匀d波超导的最小模型，但近期在现实掺杂区间的计算往往发现其他基态，且不同计算方法的内在偏置导致结果存在不确定性。作者旨在通过高精度、低偏置的变分方法厘清该模型在相关参数区的真实基态序。

Method: 采用最先进的神经张量网络（neural tensor network）和神经回流态（neural backflow state）模拟，并借助大规模GPU计算，在最多1024个格点的晶格上实现前所未有的变分精度；对纯Hubbard模型及加入次近邻跃迁（与空穴掺杂铜氧化物相关的参数）的体系在1/8掺杂下进行研究。

Result: 在1/8掺杂的纯Hubbard模型以及含次近邻跃迁的体系中，均匀d波态、超导条纹态与非超导条纹态之间呈现出毫t（milli-t）能量尺度上的近简并。

Conclusion: 本文重新平衡了"Hubbard模型在相关参数区支持单一主导序"的叙事，转而描绘出条纹与超导之间极端竞争的图景。

Abstract: The two-dimensional Hubbard model was originally proposed as the minimal model of uniform $d$-wave superconductivity in the cuprate high-temperature superconductors, but recent calculations with relevant parameters in a realistic doping regime have often found other ground states. Further, uncertainty remains over such results due to the intrinsic bias of different computational methods. Here we address this uncertainty through state-of-the-art neural tensor network and neural backflow state simulations, enabled by large-scale GPU computation, that together reach an unprecedented variational accuracy on lattices with up to 1024 sites. At $1/8$ doping in the pure Hubbard model, as well as with next-nearest-neighbor hopping relevant to the hole-doped cuprates, we reveal a near degeneracy among a low-energy uniform $d$-wave state, superconducting stripes, and non-superconducting stripe states on the milli-$t$ energy scale. Our work rebalances the narrative that the Hubbard model supports a single dominant order in relevant parameter regimes towards a picture of extreme competition between stripes and superconductivity.

</details>


### [7] [Emergent altermagnetism in (LaVO$_3$)$_n$/(LaNiO$_3$)$_m$ superlattices from interfacial charge transfer and octahedral tilts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30371)
*Gustavo H. Cassemiro,Walber H. Brito*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算表明，在(LaVO₃)ₙ/(LaNiO₃)ₘ超晶格中，界面电荷转移与八面体倾斜的协同作用可诱发交变磁性（altermagnetism），无需自旋轨道耦合即可产生d波自旋劈裂，为氧化物异质结构设计交变磁体提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁性作为一种新兴的自旋有序相，其自旋劈裂通常依赖自旋轨道耦合或特定对称性，而在材料体系中实现并调控它仍具挑战性。作者希望利用氧化物异质结构的界面工程（电荷转移与八面体畸变）来主动设计并调控交变磁性，而非依赖天然单晶材料。

Method: 采用第一性原理DFT+U计算，构建(LaVO₃)ₙ/(LaNiO₃)ₘ超晶格（(n,m)=(1,1)、(2,2)、(2,4)），分析界面电荷转移导致的价态重构（V⁴⁺/Ni²⁺）、C型反铁磁序、八面体倾斜模式（a⁻b⁺c⁻或a⁻b⁻c⁻）及其非点式对称性联系，并定量计算无自旋轨道耦合情况下的d波自旋劈裂与各轨道（V、Ni、O 2p）的贡献权重。

Result: 1) 界面电荷转移将原本顺磁的镍酸盐层转变为磁性活跃子晶格，形成C型反铁磁序。2) C型反铁磁序与a⁻b⁺c⁻或a⁻b⁻c⁻八面体倾斜共同作用，使相反自旋子晶格仅通过非点式操作联系，从而在不含自旋轨道耦合时产生d波自旋劈裂。3) 绝缘的(LVO)₁/(LNO)₁超晶格中劈裂达118 meV，约为块体LaVO₃的两倍，且随镍酸盐层增厚而减小。4) 劈裂由V和Ni子晶格共同承载，其中近一半权重来自氧2p态（经p-d共价杂化）。

Conclusion: 界面电荷转移可作为在氧化物异质结构中设计交变磁性的普适原则：通过调控界面电荷转移和八面体倾斜，可在无自旋轨道耦合条件下实现并增强d波自旋劈裂，为交变磁体的工程化设计铺平道路。

Abstract: Using first-principles calculations, we show that altermagnetism emerges in (LaVO$_3$)$_n$/(LaNiO$_3$)$_m$ superlattices, with $(n,m) = (1,1), (2,2)$ and $(2,4)$, from the interplay of interfacial charge transfer and octahedral tilts. Charge transfer drives the system towards the V$^{4+}$ and Ni$^{2+}$ configuration, turning the otherwise paramagnetic nickelate layers into a magnetically active sublattice. The resulting $C$-type antiferromagnetic order, combined with $a^-b^+c^-$ or $a^-b^-c^-$ octahedral tilts, links opposite-spin sublattices only through nonsymmorphic operations, yielding a $d$-wave spin splitting without spin-orbit coupling. Within DFT+U, the splitting reaches 118 meV in the insulating (LVO)$_1$/(LNO)$_1$ superlattice, about twice that calculated for bulk LaVO$_3$, and decreases as the nickelate layers become thicker. Our findings indicate that this splitting is hosted by both the V and Ni sublattices, with nearly half of its weight on oxygen $2p$ states through $p$-$d$ covalency. Interfacial charge transfer thus emerges as a \textit{design} principle for engineering altermagnetism in oxide heterostructures.

</details>


### [8] [Unrestricted Binary Tree State for the Fermionic Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30399)
*Mahroo Mirabdolhagh Hazaveh,Armin Khamoshi,Fei Gao,Thomas M. Henderson,Gustavo E. Scuseria*

This provides a mechanism for a paired ansatz to describe the singly occupied configurations that dominate the repulsive Hubbard model at large U/t. We find that unrestricted BTS closely approaches optimized DOCI in the repulsive regime, substantially improves upon AGP, and reproduces DOCI density correlations more accurately than pCCD

When an idealized spatially inhomogeneous strain field around the Ru lamellae is included, the observed sign and qualitative magnitude of the anisotropy are reproduced more closely than in a simple unstrained two-component model. Abstract: The intrinsic anomalous Hall effect (AHE), originating from Berry curvature, has been extensively studied in $f$-electron systems, including Ce-based compounds. Here, we investigate this issue in CeRu$_2$Si$_2$ by calculating the intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) for several occupied Ce-$4f$ states using first-principles LDA+$U$ calculations combined with Wannier interpolation method. We show that localized $4f$-electron magnetism induces exchange splitting in the conduction bands, generating Berry curvature in specific regions near the Fermi surface. This mechanism enables conduction bands that are derived from nonmagnetic orbitals and form the Fermi surface to contribute to the intrinsic AHE, suggesting a route to realizing large intrinsic AHE in $f$-electron materials.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMEA作为非平衡稳态DMFT的非微扰杂质求解器，其精度受限于辅助系统对物理杂化函数的拟合质量。由于杂化函数变化最快部分由分布函数决定，且其费米边在低温下变陡，拟合在弱库耦合时倾向于高估温度，从而引入伪加热误差。作者希望在不牺牲AMEA高精度的前提下，修正这一由拟合引起的误差。

Conclusion: 该拟合误差修正有效弥补了AMEA在低温弱耦合情形下因杂化函数拟合不精确而高估温度的缺陷，同时保留AMEA对强关联效应（如Mott绝缘体）的精确描述，显著提升了AMEA在非平衡DMFT中的可靠性与适用范围。

Abstract: The auxiliary master equation approach (AMEA) is a nonperturbative impurity solver for steady-state nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT). It replaces the physical hybridization function by that of a small auxiliary open quantum system, whose parameters are fitted to reproduce it, and then solves the resulting many-body problem essentially exactly. AMEA's accuracy is thus limited by the quality of this fit: since the fastest-varying part of the hybridization is set by the distribution function, whose Fermi edges sharpen at low temperature, the fit tends to overestimate the temperature, especially for weak reservoir coupling. For the auxiliary hybridization itself, however, AMEA's self-energy is essentially exact. We correct for the fit error by estimating, via an approximate solver applicable to arbitrary hybridization functions, the ratio between the self-energies of the physical and auxiliary hybridizations, and using it to extend AMEA's self-energy from the auxiliary to the physical case. This fit-error correction becomes exact whenever either the fit or the approximate solver is exact. We benchmark the scheme using the nonequilibrium Kajueter-Kotliar iterated perturbation theory (IPT), generalized here to spin-polarized solutions, as the approximate solver; other approximate solvers, such as the noncrossing approximation, can be used as well. Applied to an Anderson impurity model with a spin-polarized bath, and within DMFT to a Hubbard layer weakly coupled to two metallic reservoirs, the correction largely removes the spurious heating from the AMEA fit and from insufficient thermalization by the reservoirs, while preserving the correct description of the Mott insulator, which IPT alone cannot fully recover.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate magnetic correlations in the two-leg Kondo-Hubbard ladder using the density-matrix renormalization group method. We focus on a parameter regime in which the noninteracting electronic system has a narrow indirect band gap. In this regime, there is no Fermi surface, and the conventional Fermi-surface-based Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida picture is not directly applicable; magnetic correlations between localized spins become weaker as the noninteracting band gap increases. We find that a moderate on-site Coulomb interaction enhances the antiferromagnetic correlations between localized spins. This enhancement, which is also seen in the staggered magnetization induced by a pinning field at the edges, occurs for both ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic Kondo couplings. Our results show that electron-electron interactions can enhance magnetic correlations between localized spins even when the underlying noninteracting electronic structure is gapped, suggesting an interaction-driven route to magnetic instabilities in narrow-gap Kondo-lattice systems.

</details>

Abstract: Collective ordering in correlated electron systems emerges from microscopic dynamics, yet the evolution of observable textures need not admit a closed, memoryless description. We introduce a graph-recurrent neural network (GRNN) framework that learns such emergent dynamics directly from trajectories without specifying microscopic equations of motion. The framework integrates gated recurrent memory with graph message passing: local hidden states encode information beyond the instantaneous observables, while spatial coupling allows this information to propagate between sites. The observable and hidden fields evolve jointly through a shared local rule, providing a scalable representation of dynamics with spatial interactions and temporal memory. We demonstrate this framework on the emergent dynamics of charge-density-wave order in the semiclassical Holstein model, successfully reproducing temporal and spatial correlations and dynamical scaling during domain coarsening. By learning finite-time evolution directly, the GRNN offers a route to efficient, data-driven descriptions of collective dynamics with unresolved microscopic degrees of freedom.

</details>

Method: 作者发展了玻色子辅助场量子蒙特卡洛（BAFQMC）方法，并严格证明时间反演对称性和反射正性各自为无符号问题模拟提供一般性的充分条件；在该判据下，即便在阻挫晶格上也能规避符号问题。

Abstract: The sign problem poses a fundamental barrier to numerically exact quantum Monte Carlo simulations, severely hindering our understanding of quantum many-body physics across disciplines ranging from condensed matter to particle and nuclear physics. Geometrical frustration makes this barrier especially acute for interacting bosons. Here, we develop a bosonic auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo (BAFQMC) method and rigorously prove that time-reversal symmetry and reflection positivity each provide a general sufficient condition for sign-problem-free simulations. Crucially, under either criterion, BAFQMC evades the sign problem even on frustrated lattices where conventional world-line and stochastic-series-expansion methods fail. Benchmarking our approach on a two-flavor Bose-Hubbard model on the triangular lattice yields excellent quantitative agreement with finite-temperature exact diagonalization. By establishing unifying principles to solve the sign problem in BAFQMC, our work substantially expands the reach of unbiased, numerically exact simulations for frustrated bosonic matter.

</details>

Abstract: We demonstrate reconfigurable, field-free superconducting diode behavior in NbN/FePt and NbN/AlN/FePt heterostructures with remanent stripe domains in the FePt layer. Under applied perpendicular magnetic fields, the interplay between the external field and the magnetic stray fields modifies the flux-flow instability and the apparent instability velocity inferred from transport, with values below those reported for bare NbN films. These results show that stripe-domain ferromagnetic overlayers provide a reconfigurable route to control superconducting nonreciprocity and flux-flow stability in NbN-based heterostructures.

Abstract: We present the conditions for satisfying the cluster property in the superconducting state. This provides a resolution to the long-standing concern raised by Leggett et al.\ regarding particle-number fluctuation in the superconducting state. In addition, by considering the entanglement between two particles composed of the maximum geminal of the BCS ground state [J.\ Phys. Commun. 5, 095003 (2021)], it is confirmed that the larger the deviation from the conditions for satisfying the cluster property becomes, the greater the magnitude of the entanglement becomes. Abstract: Motivated by recent experiments on Ca$_2$CuO$_2$Cl$_2$, we present a comprehensive study of the interplay between dopants and localized Zhang-Rice singlets in lightly hole-doped cuprates. When tunneling matrix-element effects are included, the bound states exhibit a characteristic $4a_0 \times 2a_0$ internal structure, consistent with experimental observations. Our results suggest that Coulomb interactions between dopants and localized Zhang-Rice singlets, together with tunneling matrix-element effects, provide a microscopic mechanism for the observed $4a_0$ electronic structures.

Abstract: A stationary many-electron eigenstate has one dynamical phase although its internal response can be strongly multimode. We ask whether the stationary Hartree--Fock information carrier contains enough information to determine the scalar correlation energy through one effective return. In regularized Hartree--Fock, Gaussian primitive products fix a minimum-information relative-coordinate mode before any one-mode reduction; virial completion defines the correlation source, while exact contact measures the additional return restriction created by neighboring occupied pairs. The Be-like $1s^22s^2$ sequence establishes the one-valence-pair boundary: with no neighboring occupied valence pair, the source returns through the universal floor $D_0=\sqrt{1+δ^2}$. Ten-electron molecules and Ne-like ions then test how finite-pair contact dresses that return. The same parameter-free source--return construction reproduces the established molecular and ionic correlation trends with quantitative accuracy. The numerical agreement therefore serves as validation of a physical separation between source strength and return restriction, rather than as the objective by itself.

</details>

Abstract: We consider boundary conditions of two-dimensional Dirac fermions which preserve a non-anomalous continuous symmetry which acts differently on the left- and right-movers. Such boundaries are equivalent to topological defects of Weyl fermions preserving the symmetry. We give a general construction of such defects for conformally-embedded symmetries using Chern-Simons theory. They always exist and can be realised as half-space gauging defects for some finite categorical symmetry. Using this technology, we compute the twist lines to which fermions become attached upon scattering through the defect, the boundary central charge of the corresponding folded boundary state, and its mod-2 valued SPT class. As applications, we first consider abelian symmetries and find agreement with known results in the literature. We then study a novel example where an $SU(2) \times SU(4) \times U(1)^2$ symmetry is preserved. In contrast to the abelian case, the twist lines are intrinsically non-invertible and belong to either a $\text{TY}(\mathbb{Z}_3 \times \mathbb{Z}_3)$ or $\text{Rep}(\mathbb{S}_3) \times \mathbb{Z}_3$ symmetry depending on whether scattering is to the left or the right.

Abstract: We develop a simple Lorentz-covariant construction of the Born--Infeld-type cubic interactions $(s, s_1, s_2)$ of massless higher-spin fields with $s + s_1 + s_2$ derivatives in four-dimensiona flat space. In contrast to the famous Berends--Burgers--van Dam currents, these currents are constructed from higher-spin Weyl tensors of a single chirality, schematically $J \sim \partial C \partial C$. Starting with a general ansatz, we derive recurrence relations fixing the current coefficients, identify the unique non-trivial conserved current after eliminating trivial (fake) vertices, and show that in the range $s \le s_1 + s_2$ the interaction admits a manifestly gauge-invariant representation in terms of higher-spin Weyl tensors. The construction is then extended to $\mathcal{N}=1$ and $\mathcal{N}=2$ supersymmetry using the corresponding off-shell superfield formalisms. In the $\mathcal{N}=1$ case, we obtain the general conserved integer superspin $s$ supercurrent with manifest supersymmetry, which is bilinear in the Weyl supertensors of superspins $Y_1$ and $Y_2$. For identical superfields, $Y_1 = Y_2$, this supercurrent vanishes for odd $s$ and is in agreement with earlier results by Gates and Koutrolikos. In the $\mathcal{N}=2$ case, using harmonic superspace, we construct the principal higher-spin supercurrent whose coefficients are uniquely fixed by the conservation equations. In contrast to the $\mathcal{N}=0,1$ cases, for identical $\mathcal{N}=2$ superfields, the corresponding vertex does not vanish. The results provide a systematic framework for Born--Infeld-type higher-spin interactions and their supersymmetric extensions.

Abstract: The completeness hypothesis states that quantum gravity realizes every representation allowed by its gauge group. Previous S-matrix arguments used the graviton pole to constrain exchanged representations, including a proof of completeness by imposing an additional high-energy bound on crossing-odd amplitudes. We investigate what follows without this assumption, using standard twice-subtracted dispersion relations, positivity, and crossing symmetry. For external massless SU(2) isospin $J$, we prove ``half-completeness'': either massive exchanges include every isospin $j\le J$, or at least one $j>J$ is required. At $J=1$, we construct an explicit amplitude with only $j=0,1$ exchanges that satisfies these conditions but violates the crossing-odd bound. This establishes that the additional high-energy assumption does not follow from the standard framework. For $J\ge2$, however, we show that lower-only spectra cannot be realized within a class of string-inspired Euler amplitudes, leaving their existence an open question. More generally, for scalar matter transforming under any compact internal symmetry group, we prove that massive singlets of unbounded Lorentz spin must be exchanged.

Abstract: Stochastic forcing can prevent diversity collapse in competitive dynamical systems, yet the quantitative conditions and the dynamical mechanism responsible remain poorly characterized. We address both questions in a class of nonlinear stochastic replicator dynamics on the N-dimensional probability simplex, parameterized by a nonlinearity exponent gamma and additive Gaussian noise of intensity sigma. Under the baseline interaction, the minimum noise required to sustain diversity decays as a power law, sigma_c ~ N^(-alpha); replacing the baseline interaction with a superlinear variant shifts alpha from about 1.1 to about 2.0, in agreement with a linear-stability prediction alpha = (gamma+2)/2, identifying alpha as a model-specific quantity rather than a universal index. Scanning the (gamma, sigma) plane reveals a smooth dynamical crossover: the fluctuation susceptibility chi_H = N Var(H) peaks at gamma ~ 0.7 (chi_H ~ 0.6) and is suppressed at high gamma, with no divergence near gamma ~ 1.4; matched finite-size analysis finds no systematic divergence of the peak with N, ruling out a genuine second-order phase transition. The entropy relaxation time tau_relax(gamma) remains approximately constant (about 2.9 time units) across the full range of gamma, even as chi_H decreases sixfold; this decoupling between relaxation timescale and fluctuation amplitude implies that suppression arises from convergence of diverse pathways to similar endpoints rather than from accelerated relaxation along a single channel. These findings identify nonlinear fluctuation suppression via pathway multiplicity -- distinct from both noise-induced transitions and equilibrium criticality -- as the operative stabilization pattern in this class of nonequilibrium stochastic systems.

</details>

Abstract: Distinguishing rapid local energy exchange from sustained transport matters when thermal conductivity is inferred from finite simulations. In a weakly coupled chain of separable quartic oscillators, we identify a quantitative mechanism that changes an early Green--Kubo plateau: resonant neighbors exchange energy and coherently return it. The pair heat-current integral maps to the classical pendulum trapping response. Quartic homogeneity gives an energy-independent reference period $T_{\rm lib}=7.77ε^{-1/2}$, where $ε$ is the coupling. Without fitted parameters, the leading response predicts a negative first minimum at $0.952T_{\rm lib}$ with depth $-0.74$ times the independent-cell exchange coefficient. Isolated-pair simulations test this reference and resolve finite-coupling corrections. The chain reaches its first minimum on the same time scale, with a shallower minimum and a small later residual. Comparisons with chaotic cells and energy-conserving noise show regimes in which coherent return is absent or interrupted over the measured windows. These finite-time results provide a quantitative reference for separating the early exchange plateau from subsequent correlated dynamics.

Abstract: Nonreciprocal interactions can break detailed balance by sustaining stationary probability currents. Here we ask whether such currents can also emerge when the agents' immediate coordination preferences are reciprocal, but their state-adjustment rules are determined by forward-looking optimization (foresight). We study a minimal stochastic linear-quadratic game in which two agents control noisy scalar states and minimize individual discounted costs in a full-information feedback Nash equilibrium. The optimized controls yield an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process whose stationary probability current and entropy-production rate, measuring statistical time-irreversibility, can be calculated exactly. For reciprocal coordination with unequal self-anchoring strengths, the effective drift asymmetry first appears at order $τ^5$, where $τ$ is the planning timescale, and the normalized entropy production consequently scales as $τ^8$ in the limit $τ\to0$. We derive this cancellation from the coupled Nash Riccati equations. For conformist-contrarian coupling, normalized entropy production approaches a nonzero short-foresight limit and initially decreases from that limit by an amount proportional to $τ^2$. We also determine the long-foresight limit for a conformist-contrarian pair with equal self-anchoring strengths. Numerical solutions of the full Nash equations verify the asymptotic laws and reveal the effect of foresight. Thus, forward-looking optimization can generate or reduce normalized stationary irreversibility, depending on the underlying interaction.

Abstract: Einstein's theory of diffusion defines what is considered {\it normal diffusion}. Historically, Einstein considered homogeneous diffusion characterised by a diffusion constant in his original calculations, resulting in what is known as {\it Fickian} diffusion, which thermally relaxes concentration inhomogeneities. However, in the same derivation of Einstein, the seed of generalisation of the theory to {\it non-Fickian} diffusion in the presence of space-dependent diffusivity is hidden. This indicates that non-Fickian diffusion, which can drive concentration inhomogeneities, can also be normal diffusion. The present article discusses the origin of the {\it normal} non-Fickian diffusion in Einstein's theory of diffusion.

In the range of domain sizes covered, descriptors of radius one account for more than half of this difference, and stackings of identical strips have thresholds linear in the fraction of each boundary type. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现实搜索过程在有限资源预算下运行（例如环境记忆过程），但现有研究常假定资源无限或仅将其视为需最小化的成本；因此需要解决如何在约束资源条件下优化搜索效率的资源配置问题。

Method: 研究一个受资源约束的一维扩散代理，比较三种资源模型：(1) 外部控制器施加外力将搜索者拉向目标；(2) 搜索者沿路径沉积方向性标记以重定向运动；(3) 自生化学排斥线索形成力场。对比"有信息"策略（利用当前与历史位置信息）与"盲策略"（遵循预定义时间协议），并分析非马尔可夫环境记忆过程的影响。

Result: 当掌握搜索者当前及过去位置信息时，资源应集中用于空间中的窄小区域；而在盲策略中，应以恒定速率使用资源，确保在到达目标时资源已按最大容量使用完毕。此外，具有环境记忆的非马尔可夫过程中的盲策略，因沉积线索位置的固有涨落而效率显著降低。

Abstract: Improving the efficiency of a stochastic search often requires to provide the searching agent with resources that modify its dynamics and help it explore space more quickly. Although such resources are often assumed to be unlimited and treated as a cost to mitigate, many realistic search processes operate under a finite resource budget, e.g. in processes with environmental memory in which the searcher deposits long-lasting cues along its path that it can interact with at later times to guide its further search. Here, we address the question of how to allocate a limited budget to maximize search efficiency by investigating a one-dimensional diffusing agent whose access to resources is constrained. We consider three resource models, namely an external force applied by an external controller to pull the searcher towards the target, directional flags deposited by the searcher along its path that redirect its motion towards the target, and self-generated chemorepulsive cues that act as a force field. For all these models, we find that resource usage should be concentrated in narrow regions of space when information about the searcher's current and past positions is available. In contrast, in blind strategies where a predefined time protocol is followed, resources should be used at a steady rate to ensure it to be used at most of its capacity by the time it reaches the target. We further show that blind strategies in non-Markovian processes with environmental memory are significantly less efficient due to the inherent fluctuations in the locations of deposited cues.

</details>

Conclusion: 在子午环面边界条件下归一化相对 Abel BF 挠率与 Alexander 多项式精确等同，并可由单一素数分圆挠率值在紧亏格界内恢复全部系数，显示出与连通和、分支覆盖的良好相容性。然而，两扇区约化的配对缺陷、界面收缩以及 Hochschild 不变量等构成标量比较与 Morita 等价的实质障碍；尽管存在保持派生配对的六生成元 dg 完美模范畴等价和经典一维 BV-BFV 实现，量子 BFV 扇区也退化为真空场，但本文未能建立完整的几何 BV-BFV/Floer 等价，遗留的障碍被明确刻画。

Abstract: We compare relative Abelian BF torsion with the Alexander-graded Euler characteristic of knot Floer homology. A meridional annulus boundary condition identifies the normalized relative torsion with the Alexander polynomial. One exact prime-cyclotomic torsion value recovers all its coefficients under a sharp genus bound, compatibly with connected-sum gluing and branched-cover formulas. We compute interface contractions and obstructions to scalar comparisons, including the pairing defect of two-sector reduction for the degree-zero torus algebra. A six-generator dg resolution yields an equivalence of perfect-module categories preserving derived pairings and admits a classical one-dimensional BV-BFV realization. In the fixed nonresonant polynomial quantum BFV sector, over characteristic zero with $\hbar$ inverted, both states and Weyl observables reduce to a vacuum field; a Hochschild invariant obstructs a Morita equivalence with the degree-zero torus algebra. These results do not establish a full geometric BV-BFV/Floer equivalence.

Moreover, we also characterize the optimizers of these inequalities. In particular, the optimizing density profiles form a two-dimensional manifold, which we call here the Poincaré density manifold.

More precisely, we first obtain an explicit and non-asymptotic bound on the global and local error made by the TFW approximation. These bounds are controlled by the distance of the normalized density profile from the Poincaré density manifold times the number of particles. In particular, these bounds show that the TFW approximation is exact on the Poincaré manifold. We then show that, in the periodic setting, the distance between the Kohn-Sham potential and the inverse TFW potential in the natural dual Sobolev space can also be controlled by explicit density dependent constants times the distance to the Poincaré manifold. As a corollary, we prove that for a fixed density profile, the Kohn-Sham potential converges towards the inverse Thomas--Fermi potential in the high-density limit $N \rightarrow \infty$ with asymptotic rate of order $N^{-2}$. This rate is shown to be optimal.

Abstract: We study the symmetry reduction of optimal control problems using Dirac structures. The first-order conditions of the Pontryagin Maximum Principle are written as an implicit Dirac system on the Pontryagin bundle. For a problem invariant under a Lie group, we reduce both the Lagrange-Pontryagin variational principle and the underlying Dirac structure, and prove that they yield the same reduced equations, which we identify with the Hamiltonian equations on the dual of the Atiyah algebroid. Reconstruction, independence of the principal connection, and the relation between criticality and optimality complete the picture. Dropping regularity, we give the Gotay-Nester-Hinds constraint algorithm a Dirac-geometric form and prove that symmetry reduction commutes with it. For control-linear systems whose admissible directions are horizontal for a principal connection, abnormal extremals lie on the leaf where the base costate vanishes, and the first secondary constraint is the degeneracy of the curvature pairing. We also give a reduced criterion for strict abnormality and a metric-free index form. TL;DR: 本文针对经典 N 体问题，将系统主要特征的极值求解转化为相空间上的非线性规划问题，证明了条件极小值与局部极值只能出现在满足总角动量非零条件的平坦（不一定有限）轨迹上，并进一步给出使这些轨迹成为有限（圆弧或椭圆）平坦轨迹的角动量与总能量限制条件，以及在这些轨迹上系统关键特征的取值。附录中还讨论了更一般模型的相关结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 N 体问题中，系统相空间上的主要特征（如能量、角动量等）在什么轨迹上取得条件极小值或局部极值，以及这些极值轨迹具有何种几何与动力学结构，是尚未充分刻画的问题。作者希望通过非线性规划方法揭示极值轨迹的平坦性与有限性条件，从而深化对 N 体系统定性行为的理解。

Method: 将 N 体系统主要特征的极值问题表述为相空间上的空间非线性规划问题；在系统总角动量非零的假设下，利用变分/规划方法证明条件极小值和局部极值只能位于平坦（flat）轨迹上；进而分析角动量与总能量积分对轨迹的限制，确定轨迹有限（圆或椭圆）所需的条件，并计算这些平坦轨迹上系统关键特征的值。

Result: 证明了在总角动量非零条件下，系统特征的条件极小值与局部极值只能由空间中一般非有限的平坦轨迹实现；得到了限制角动量与总能量取值的基本条件，使得这些平坦轨迹为有限的圆形或椭圆形轨迹，并在相应积分限制下系统的平坦结构随时间保持不变；同时给出了这些平坦轨迹上系统关键特征的具体值。

Conclusion: 本文建立了 N 体问题相空间上系统特征极值位置与平坦轨迹之间的对应关系，明确了角动量和能量等运动积分对轨迹平坦性与有限性的约束条件，并给出了相关特征值，为经典 N 体问题的变分定性分析提供了新结果，附录还将其推广到更一般的系统模型。

Abstract: For the classical model of a system of N particles with interaction (the N-body problem) we consider some spatial problems of nonlinear programming for finding conditional minima and local extrema for the main characteristics of the system on its phase space. We prove that these conditional minima and local extrema of the system characteristics can only be achieved on flat, in general, not necessarily finite, trajectories of the system in space, (under the condition that the system angular momentum is non-zero). And then we find the basic conditions limiting the values of the integrals of motion: angular momentum and total energy of the system under which these trajectories are flat finite ("circular" or elliptical) trajectories on which the flat structure of the system is preserved in time under the certain restrictions on the values of the system's integrals of motion. And we determine also the values of the system's key characteristics on these flat trajectories. Next, in the Appendix, we consider some additional related results for a more general model of the system.

</details>


### [84] [Quantum Charge as a Particle/Antiparticle Label](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29791)
*Alexander D. Popov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了粒子/反粒子概念源于 Weyl-Heisenberg 群 WH₃ 的 U(1)ᵥ 子群，粒子与反粒子对应于相反的"量子荷"（±1/ħ），并证明 Klein-Gordon 与 Dirac 理论中的负概率/负能量和 Klein 佯谬源于 U(1)ᵥ 不变性的破坏，恢复该不变性即可消除这些佯谬。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在为粒子与反粒子的区别提供一个群论（几何/代数）层面的统一解释，并将其追溯为希尔伯特空间上不可公约化表示的性质，而非方程的结果；同时希望从根源上解决一次量子化相对论理论中长期存在的负能量、负概率与 Klein 佯谬等问题。

Method: 以 Weyl-Heisenberg 群 WH₃ 为框架，其生成元为位置算符 x̂ᵃ、动量算符 p̂ₐ 及定义 U(1)ᵥ 子群的中心元素 Qᵥ。通过 Qᵥ 的本征值 q₊=1/ħ 与 q₋=-1/ħ 定义"量子荷"，将波函数分为粒子 ψ₊ 与反粒子 ψ₋，它们分别属于希尔伯特空间 H₊ 与 H₋，其上承载着 WH₃ 的两个单位不等价不可约表示 π_ħ 与 π_{-ħ}。引入反线性电荷共轭算符 Ĉ，将 H₊(q₊) 映射到 H₋(q₋)。指出量子荷是波函数的固有属性，先于任何动力学方程而存在；并论证 Klein-Gordon 与 Dirac 理论中的困难源于违反 U(1)ᵥ 不变性。

Result: 确立了粒子/反粒子与 WH₃ 群 U(1)ᵥ 子群量子荷（±1/ħ）之间的对应关系；证明波函数在任意方程给出前即固有地携带 q₊ 或 q₋ 电荷；成功说明 Klein-Gordon 与 Dirac 理论中的负概率/负能量及 Klein 佯谬均是 U(1)ᵥ 不变性被破坏的后果，恢复该不变性后粒子与反粒子分离、所有佯谬在一次量子化层面被消除。

Conclusion: 粒子与反粒子的区分是 Weyl-Heisenberg 群表示理论的内在特征，由 U(1)ᵥ 子群的量子荷 ±1/ħ 决定，且先于动力学方程存在；一次量子化相对论理论中的各项佯谬可通过对 U(1)ᵥ 对称性的正确恢复而彻底消解。

Abstract: We show that the concept of particles and antiparticles is related to the Weyl-Heisenberg group $W\!H_3$. Its generators are the position operators $\hat x^a$, the momentum operators $\hat p_a$, and the central element $Q_{\sf{v}}$, which defines a subgroup U(1)$_{\sf v}$ of $W\!H_3$. We show that the wave functions have a charge relative to the group U(1)$_{\sf{v}}$ equal to either $q_+=1/\hbar$ (particle $ψ_+$) or $q_-=-1/\hbar$ (antiparticle $ψ_-$). We call these eigenvalues of the operator $Q_{\sf{v}}$ quantum charges: $Q_{\sf{v}}ψ_\pm^{}=q_\pm^{}ψ_\pm^{}$. The wave functions $ψ_+$ and $ψ_-$ belong to the Hilbert spaces $\mathcal{H}_+$ and $\mathcal{H}_-$, on which unitarily inequivalent representations $π_\hbar$ and $π_{-\hbar}$ of the Weyl-Heisenberg group $W\!H_3$ are defined. These representations are related to each other by the antilinear charge conjugation operator $\hat C$, which maps $\mathcal{H}_+, q_+$ to $\mathcal{H}_-, q_-$. Charges $q_\pm^{}$ are intrinsic characteristics of the wave functions $ψ_\pm^{}$, similar to spin and electric charge. The wave function possesses one of these charges, $q_+$ or $q_-$, prior to the specification of any equations. We show that negative probabilities/energies and the Klein paradox in Klein-Gordon and Dirac theories are consequences of the violation of U(1)$_{\sf v}$-invariance in these theories. Restoring this invariance separates particle and antiparticles and eliminates all paradoxes at the level of first-quantized relativistic theories.

</details>

### [85] [Electronic Screening as a Design Lever in La-Doped BaTiO$_3$: Coupled Polarization, Dielectric Response, and Optical Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30210)
*Sikandar Azam,Muhammad Fareed,Qaiser Rafiq,Jan Minar,Abed Alataway*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算研究了La施主掺杂对BaTiO3铁电、介电、电子及光学性质的影响，发现12.5%的La掺杂通过增强电子屏蔽、硬化极性声子模式、降低异常玻恩有效电荷，导致极化抑制、静态介电响应下降、吸收边蓝移及光学双折射减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施主掺杂可通过改变晶格畸变与电子屏蔽来调控钙钛矿氧化物的铁电、介电、电子和光学等耦合性质，但其中的微观机理尚需系统阐明。作者旨在揭示La掺杂BaTiO3中掺杂诱导屏蔽、声子硬化和极化抑制之间的内在联系。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）、密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT）以及用于计算极化的Berry相位方法，构建Ba位12.5% La掺杂的BaTiO3模型，系统计算结构、声子、介电、电子结构及光学性质。

Result: La掺杂引起各向异性晶格收缩，降低四方性和Ti偏心位移，削弱四方铁电相的能量稳定性；极化降低伴随电子屏蔽增强和异常玻恩有效电荷减小；声子计算表明弛豫后的掺杂结构动力学稳定，低频极性模式硬化与铁电性减弱一致；高频介电响应适度增大，而静态介电响应因晶格贡献受抑而减小；电子结构显示费米能级向导电带移动且无带隙内缺陷态；光学吸收边蓝移符合Burstein-Moss机制，近边透明性增强，双折射减小。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了施主诱导屏蔽、极性模式硬化、动力学电荷减小、极化抑制与光学各向异性降低之间的微观联系。以上结论仅适用于研究的12.5%掺杂浓度，需要更多浓度的计算以建立定量的掺杂趋势。

Abstract: Donor substitution can tune the coupled ferroelectric, dielectric, electronic, and optical properties of perovskite oxides by modifying lattice distortions and electronic screening. We investigate these effects in La-doped BaTiO3 using density functional theory, density-functional perturbation theory, and the Berry-phase formalism for polarization. For a model with 12.5% La substitution at the Ba site, La incorporation induces anisotropic lattice contraction, reduces tetragonality and Ti off-centering, and weakens the energetic stabilization of the tetragonal ferroelectric phase. The accompanying reduction in polarization is associated with enhanced electronic screening and reduced anomalous Born effective charges. Phonon calculations indicate that the relaxed doped structure is dynamically stable, while hardening of a low-frequency polar mode is consistent with weakened ferroelectricity. The high-frequency dielectric response increases moderately, whereas the static dielectric response decreases as the lattice contribution is suppressed. Electronic-structure calculations show a Fermi-level shift toward the conduction band without mid-gap defect states. The optical spectra exhibit an absorption-edge shift consistent with a Burstein-Moss-type mechanism, enhanced near-edge transparency, and reduced birefringence. Together, these results suggest a microscopic connection between donor-induced screening, polar-mode hardening, reduced dynamical charges, polarization suppression, and decreased optical anisotropy. The conclusions apply to the investigated 12.5% composition; calculations at additional concentrations are needed to establish quantitative doping trends.

</details>


### [86] [Domain walls in $R3c$-structured NaNbO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30318)
*Jack G. Nedell,Craig J. Fennie*

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理系统研究了钙钛矿氧化物NaNbO₃低温菱方R3c相中的结构畴壁，基于群论分类提出八类畴壁，发现其中一类109°畴壁能量极低（9 mJ m⁻²），并据此提出R3c↔Pbcm相变的原子机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NaNbO₃低温R3c相中畴壁的结构、能量及其与相变的关系尚缺乏系统的理论认识。作者希望借助群论分类与第一性原理弛豫，厘清该相中畴壁的类别、稳定性与能量，并解释实验中观察到的晶体孪晶现象，进而揭示R3c与Pbcm相之间相变的微观机理。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，结合群论方法对R3c相的结构畴壁进行分类。根据弹性相容性与电荷中性条件唯一确定其中四类畴壁的取向，并对每一类代表性畴壁进行结构弛豫，计算其畴壁能量并分析其与实验孪晶观测的对应关系。

Result: 在R3c结构中识别出八类独特的畴壁，其中四类的取向由弹性和电中性条件唯一确定。结构弛豫表明，一类109°畴壁具有极低的能量（9 mJ m⁻²），可与BaTiO₃中的低能铁电畴壁相比。该109°畴壁与实验中观测到的R3c相晶体孪晶相符。

Conclusion: 低能109°畴壁是NaNbO₃ R3c相中能量上最有利的畴壁之一，能够解释实验观测到的孪晶结构，并为R3c↔Pbcm相变提供了原子尺度的机制。该研究强调了群论分类结合第一性原理计算在解析复杂钙钛矿畴壁与相变中的有效性。

Abstract: In this work, we undertake a systematic first-principles study of structural domain walls in the low-temperature rhombohedral $R3c$ phase of perovskite oxide NaNbO$_3$. Employing a group-theoretic classification, we introduce eight unique classes of domain walls in the $R3c$ structure, four of which have a domain wall orientation determined uniquely by the conditions of elastic compatibility and charge neutrality, and perform structural relaxations for each representative domain wall class. We find that one class of 109° domain walls has a notably low energy of 9 mJ m$^{-2}$, comparable to the low-energy ferroelectric walls in BaTiO$_3$. Finally, we find that this class of 109° walls is compatible with the crystal twinning observed in experiments on the $R3c$ phase, and propose an atomistic mechanism for the $R3c \leftrightarrow Pbcm$ phase transition.

</details>


### [87] [The often overlooked role of sound in liquid thermal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31127)
*Kamran Behnia*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文回顾了百年前提出的液体热导率 Bridgman 公式，该公式假定声速与热导率成正比，将声子视为液体中的热载流子。文章追溯其起源、与流体力学欧拉方程及抛物线热方程悖论的联系，并探讨其在量子-经典过渡中的命运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bridgman 公式在经验上很成功，但其重要性长期被低估，概念含义也被忽视，甚至未被写入知名凝聚态物理教科书。作者希望通过系统回顾来重新审视这一公式的价值与启示。

Method: 采用文献综述与理论分析的方法，追溯 Bridgman 公式的历史起源，并将其与流体力学欧拉方程、抛物线热传导方程的根本性悖论（因果性/传播速度问题）相联系，进而在量子-经典过渡框架下考察该公式的适用性与演变。

Result: 揭示了 Bridgman 公式将声子视为液体热载流子的物理图像，阐明了其与欧拉方程和抛物线热方程悖论之间的内在关联，并梳理了该公式在量子-经典过渡区间中的表现与命运。

Conclusion: Bridgman 公式的经验成功与概念意义值得重新评价，其背后涉及液体中热输运的声子图像以及热传导方程的基础性问题，对理解量子-经典过渡中的能量输运具有重要启示。

Abstract: Formulated a hundred years ago, the Bridgman formula for thermal conductivity of liquids postulates a proportionality between sound velocity and thermal conductivity. This designates phonons as heat carriers in a liquid. Its empirical success has been under-appreciated and its conceptual implications overlooked. It is not mentioned in well-known condensed-matter textbook. Here, we review its origin, its links to hydrodynamic Euler equations and to the fundamental paradox surrounding the parabolic heat equation and its fate across the quantum-classical crossover.

</details>


### [88] [Peridynamic Thermo-Mechanical Modeling of Cracking in Tungsten under Fusion-Relevant Heat Fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30356)
*Rinkle Juneja,Pablo Seleson,Samuel Temple Reeve,Justin Weinmeister,Gary Staebler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于近场动力学（peridynamics）的建模与仿真方法，利用 CabanaPD 代码和 JUDITH 电子束装置的实验数据，预测钨（聚变堆首选面向等离子体材料）在聚变相关热流下循环辐照引起的热-力开裂与损伤，并与实验分类结果吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向等离子体材料（PFMs）在聚变堆中承受极端热流、高能粒子和强中子辐照，其中极端热流引起的热-力损伤是限制其寿命的关键因素，因此需要一种方法预测 PFMs 在聚变相关条件下的开裂和损伤。

Method: 采用基于近场动力学断裂力学代码 CabanaPD 的部件尺度热-力仿真，利用 JUDITH 电子束装置对再结晶纯钨的实验数据作为输入，研究不同基体温度和热流组合下循环电子束辐照诱导的损伤。

Result: 仿真结果与实验分类对比良好，并能够在不同热流和基体温度范围内识别开裂阈值，揭示裂纹与损伤的空间分布和时间累积。

Conclusion: 该方法可为筛选候选 PFMs 和量化其在聚变相关条件下的寿命提供途径，并给出裂纹与损伤的详细时空演化信息。

Abstract: Plasma-facing materials (PFMs) are the primary armor materials in fusion reactors, protecting reactor wall components from extreme heat fluxes, energetic particles, and intense neutron irradiation. These harsh conditions induce thermo-mechanical stresses, irradiation damage, and overall degradation in PFMs. Among these, thermo-mechanical damage induced by extreme heat flux is one of the key factors limiting the lifetime of PFMs. We present a modeling and simulation approach for predicting thermo-mechanical cracking and damage in tungsten, a leading PFM candidate, under fusion-reactor-relevant heat fluxes. Specifically, we study damage induced by cyclic electron beam (e-beam) exposure under different combinations of base temperature and heat flux. We carry out component-scale thermo-mechanical simulations using CabanaPD, a peridynamics fracture mechanics code, informed by experimental data for recrystallized pure tungsten from the JUDITH e-beam facility. The simulations compare well with the experimental classification and enable identification of cracking thresholds across a range of heat fluxes and base temperatures. These predictions can provide detailed insights into the spatial distribution and temporal accumulation of cracks and damage, while offering a pathway for screening candidate PFMs and quantifying their lifespan under fusion-relevant conditions.

</details>


### [89] [Microstructural Generative Inverse Models for Additive Manufacturing Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30404)
*Pablo Luna Falcon,Jason Mayeur,Amirkoushyar Ziabari,Vincent Paquit,Patxi Fernandez-Zelaia*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出用去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）合成增材制造（AM）金属的数字孪生取向成像显微图，并建立无分类器引导框架求解性质到结构、工艺到结构的逆向问题，从而加速AM材料的鉴定与认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属增材制造在复杂结构件制造上优势显著，但其工艺-结构-性能关系复杂，导致不确定性与风险，阻碍大规模应用。纯实验方法定量这些关系耗时且昂贵；ICME框架虽能借助CPFEM从微观结构预测力学性能，却需要难以获得的大数据集，且预测精度依赖真实的AM微观结构表征，因此急需能高效合成真实AM微观结构的手段。

Method: 采用去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）合成数字孪生取向成像显微图，并以定量证据验证生成样本的保真度；同时建立无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）框架，用于求解性质到结构、工艺到结构等复杂逆问题，并在一项与鉴定相关的材料不确定性量化问题上进行了应用展示。

Result: 实验证明DDPM能够可靠地合成数字孪生取向成像显微图，且生成样本具有高保真度；无分类器引导框架成功实现性质到结构与工艺到结构的逆向映射，并在鉴定相关的材料不确定性量化问题上展示了可行应用。

Conclusion: 本研究凸显了基于AI的生成式建模作为计算框架关键组成部分的潜力，有望加速增材制造材料的鉴定与认证进程。

Abstract: Metal additive manufacturing (AM) offers substantial advantages to the fabrication of complex structural components, but its widespread adoption is slowed due to complexities in the process-structure-property relations which is the cause for uncertainty and drives risk. Accurately quantifying these complex relations solely via experimental workflows is time consuming and costly. Integrated computational materials and engineering (ICME) frameworks address this challenge by leveraging advanced computational tools, such as crystal plasticity finite element models (CPFEM), to predict critical mechanical properties directly from microstructural data. However, these frameworks require a large datasets that are often difficult to obtain, and their predictive accuracy depends on the availability of realistic AM microstructure representations. Generative deep learning models are a promising approach towards synthesizing AM microstructures which contain complex morphological and crystallographic features. Denoising diffusion probabilistic models (DDPMs), in particular, show a strong potential to capture the inherent complexities of AM structures. In this work, we demonstrate that DDPMs can reliably synthesize digital-twin orientation-imaging micrographs, and we support this claim with quantitative evidence of the fidelity of the generated samples. Additionally, we establish a classifier-free guidance framework for solving complex inverse problems such as property-to-structure and process-to-structure mappings, and we showcase a potential application on a qualification-relevant uncertainty quantification problem. Our work highlights the potential of AI-based generative modeling as a key component of computational frameworks for accelerating AM materials qualification and certification.

</details>


### [90] [Design of Nuclear and Magnetic Scattering Length Density Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30416)
*Alexandros Barnasas,Edward J. Stuckey,Alexei A. Vorobiev,Joashaniel F. K. Cooper,Sjoerd Stendahl,Björgvin Hjörvarsson*

TL;DR: 通过设计非晶FeCoAlZr薄膜的散射长度密度分布，实现了核与磁散射长度密度的相对独立调控，从而增强中子反射测量中获得的信息。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子反射测量中，样品的散射长度密度（SLD）分布可被有意设计，以增强所关注信息的获取能力。本研究旨在验证这一设计思路的可行性。

Method: 制备了四个样品，每个样品由10个AlZr间隔层与磁性层交替重复构成；磁性层成分分别为Fe80Co10Al8Zr2、Fe70Co20Al8Zr2、Fe60Co30Al8Zr2和Fe45Co45Al8Zr2。采用X射线反射率与极化中子反射率对样品进行表征。

Result: 随着Co含量增加，FeCoAlZr合金的核SLD近似线性地从7.14×10⁻⁶ Å⁻²降至4.63×10⁻⁶ Å⁻²，而磁SLD大致保持在4.3–4.5×10⁻⁶ Å⁻²。表明成分主要调控核SLD，外加磁场原则上可进一步控制磁贡献，二者可大体独立调节。

Conclusion: 研究证明核散射长度密度与磁散射长度密度可被相对独立地控制：成分主要用于调节核SLD，而外加磁场则为磁SLD提供额外调控手段，从而可按需设计样品以提升中子反射测量的信息质量。

Abstract: The scattering length density (SLD) profile of a sample can be deliberately designed to enhance the information of interest obtained from neutron reflectometry. Here, we demonstrate this approach using amorphous $\mathrm{Fe}_{x}\mathrm{Co}_{y}\mathrm{Al}_{8}\mathrm{Zr}_{2}$ thin films. Four samples, each comprising ten repetitions of an AlZr spacer layer and a magnetic layer, were prepared with magnetic-layer compositions of $\mathrm{Fe}_{80}\mathrm{Co}_{10}\mathrm{Al}_{8}\mathrm{Zr}_{2}$, $\mathrm{Fe}_{70}\mathrm{Co}_{20}\mathrm{Al}_{8}\mathrm{Zr}_{2}$, $\mathrm{Fe}_{60}\mathrm{Co}_{30}\mathrm{Al}_{8}\mathrm{Zr}_{2}$, and $\mathrm{Fe}_{45}\mathrm{Co}_{45}\mathrm{Al}_{8}\mathrm{Zr}_{2}$, and characterised using X-ray and polarised neutron reflectometry. Across the series, the nuclear SLD of the FeCoAlZr alloy decreases approximately linearly from $7.14 \times 10^{-6}$ to $4.63 \times 10^{-6}$ $\mathring{\mathrm{A}}^{-2}$ with increasing Co content, while the magnetic SLD is approximately $4.3$-$4.5 \times 10^{-6}$ $\mathring{\mathrm{A}}^{-2}$. These results demonstrate that the nuclear and magnetic contributions can be controlled largely independently, with composition primarily tuning the nuclear SLD and the applied magnetic field offering, in principle, additional control of the magnetic contribution.

  <summary>Details</summary>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状NaₓTMO₂是最有前景的钠离子正极材料之一，但其Na/空位有序、层堆叠序列及协同Jahn-Teller畸变仅在少数单过渡金属化合物中被了解，双金属体系缺乏系统认识。

Method: 将文献中报道的Na/空位有序汇总为41种唯一有序库，统一应用于5种单金属和全部10种NaₓTM₀.₅TM'₀.₅O₂氧化物；先用机器学习原子间势筛选超过12万种结构，再对1025个结构进行精确第一性原理计算；并通过Van Vleck分析每个TMO₆八面体研究Jahn-Teller畸变。

Result: 相图解释了观测到的O3/P3/O1序列与电压，O3-to-P3竞争取决于仅几meV/原子的有序能（标量描述符如阳离子势和层间板距无法捕捉）；氧化还原对形成近似与伴侣无关的阶梯Ti³⁺/⁴⁺ < Mn³⁺/⁴⁺ < Co³⁺/⁴⁺ < Ni²⁺/³⁺ < Fe³⁺/⁴⁺；TM混合通过电荷转移至Jahn-Teller非活性态或稀释活性中心而抑制Mn³⁺和Ni³⁺的协同Jahn-Teller畸变；提出能量密度-应变图设计规则，Mn⁴⁺/Ni²⁺电荷转移在NaₓMn₀.₅Ni₀.₅O₂中实现无Jahn-Teller应变的高压电对。

Conclusion: 本文提供了层状钠过渡金属氧化物的Na/空位有序、堆叠及Jahn-Teller畸变的系统认识，建立了能量密度-应变设计规则，指出Mn⁴⁺/Ni²⁺电荷转移的NaₓMn₀.₅Ni₀.₅O₂是最实用的组成，并公开了有序分辨数据集供后续训练精确机器学习原子间势使用。

Abstract: Layered sodium transition-metal (TM) oxides, Na$_x$TMO$_2$, are among the most promising sodium-ion positive electrode materials, yet their Na/vacancy orderings, layer stacking sequences, and cooperative Jahn--Teller distortions are known only for a few single-TM compounds. The phase diagrams explain the observed O3/P3/O1 sequences and voltages, and the O3-to-P3 competition hinges on ordering energies of a few meV per atom that scalar descriptors, such as the cationic potential and the interlayer-slab distance, miss. Our extensive analysis reveals that the redox couples form a nearly partner-independent ladder, Ti$^{3+/4+}$ $<$ Mn$^{3+/4+}$ $<$ Co$^{3+/4+}$ $<$ Ni$^{2+/3+}$ $<$ Fe$^{3+/4+}$, explaining pristine charge transfer and the oxidation sequence. Van Vleck analysis of every TMO$_6$ octahedron reveals that TM mixing suppresses the cooperative Jahn--Teller distortions of Mn$^{3+}$ and Ni$^{3+}$ through charge transfer to Jahn--Teller-inactive states or dilution of Jahn--Teller-active centers. We formulate an energy-density-versus-strain map that yields a design rule: a high-voltage couple without Jahn--Teller strain, which Mn$^{4+}$/Ni$^{2+}$ charge transfer realizes in Na$_x$Mn$_{0.5}$Ni$_{0.5}$O$_2$, the most practical composition. We release the ordering-resolved dataset as a training set for subsequent training of accurate machine-learning interatomic potentials.
