### [93] [Strain-induced structural transformations in hexagonal monolayer boron nitride. A density functional study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30598)
*O. Bystrenko,B. Ilkiv,S. Petrovska,O. Foia,T. Bystrenko,O. Khyzhun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算研究单层六方氮化硼（h-BN）在强形变下的结构相变，发现特定剪切应变下可形成具有pmmm空间对称性的正交相，该相为窄带隙（约0.65 eV）半导体，且热稳定至约900 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二维h-BN在强机械形变下可能的结构转变及其对电子性质的影响，寻找由宽带隙绝缘体转变为窄带隙半导体的新相。

Method: 采用基于密度泛函理论（DFT）的第一性原理计算，使用赝势方法，交换关联能取Perdew-Burke-Ernzerhof（PBE）形式；结合第一性原理Born-Oppenheimer分子动力学验证热稳定性，并考察不同方向和类型应变下的结构响应。

Result: 模拟揭示了单层h-BN在不同方向和类型应变下与弹性失效相关的多种结构转变。在特定剪切应变下可形成有序的二维正交相，其二维层空间群对称性为pmmm。该正交相热稳定性可达约900 K，电子结构计算表明其为带隙约0.65 eV的窄带隙半导体，区别于宽带隙绝缘体的传统h-BN。

Conclusion: 强剪切形变可诱导单层h-BN发生相变，生成热稳定且具有窄带隙半导体特性的pmmm正交相，为通过应变工程调控二维材料的电子性质提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this work we theoretically study the structural transformations occurring in monolayer hexagonal boron nitride (h-BN) subjected to strong deformations. The first-principle computer simulations are based on the density functional theory employing the pseudopotentials with the exchange-correlation energy given in Perdew-Burke-Ernzerhof form. Depending on the direction and the type of the strain, the simulations reveal different types of structural transformations in two-dimensional (2D) h-BN associated with the elastic failure. It is found that, under certain shear strains, there can form an ordered 2D orthorhombic phase with the 2D layer space symmetry pmmm. Thermal stability of the latter up to ~900 K is demonstrated by first-principle Born-Oppenheimer molecular dynamics. The examination of electronic structure indicates that, in contrast to the conventional h-BN, which is an insulator with the wide band gap, the predicted orthorhombic phase is expected to be a semiconductor with the narrow band gap of ~ 0.65 eV.

</details>


### [94] [How good are universal MLIPs for alloys? A benchmark is only as trustworthy as its controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30641)
*Gus L. W. Hart,Jacob N. Pursley*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文对14个通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIP）在145万个有序金属间化合物上进行基准测试，并与DFT数据库间的固有分歧对比，发现数据库分歧构成了模型误差的下限，校正参考基准后能量与稳定性排序基本一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIP）的排名通常基于其相对某个DFT数据库的形成能误差，将该数据库当作“真值”，但独立的DFT数据库之间本身就存在分歧。因此需要厘清：uMLIP的可测误差在多大程度上受限于数据库间的分歧，而非模型本身的质量。

Method: 作者选取14个uMLIP，以1,450,000个有序金属间化合物为对象，与它们均未用于训练的AFLOW数据库进行基准测试；同时对比AFLOW、Alexandria和OQMD三个独立DFT数据库之间的能量分歧，并将其作为衡量uMLIP误差的“地板”。通过重新设定参考（re-referencing）与筛除参考态被破坏的元素，重新考察能量误差与稳定性预测的排序，并检验剪切模量相关性、“坏”DFT参考数据及领先模型优势等疑似失败案例。

Result: 对约一半结构，三个数据库的分歧小于6–8 meV/atom，但少数结构分歧远大于此，使平均分歧达到16–30 meV/atom，该分歧构成任何模型可测误差的下限。重新参考后，最佳uMLIP与AFLOW的一致程度与Alexandria相当，次优四个模型位于该地板之上2–7 meV/atom。筛除参考态被破坏的元素后，能量排序与稳定性排序几乎处处一致。原先看似的三类模型失败——剪切模量相关性差、数千个“坏”DFT参考数据、以及领先模型被夸大的优势——在适当对照下缩小或消失。

Conclusion: uMLIP的评估不能脱离DFT数据库之间的固有分歧；数据库分歧是模型可测误差的硬性下限。在正确的参考基准和对照条件下，各uMLIP的能量与稳定性表现高度一致，许多表面上的模型失效实为参考数据或评估方式所致。

Abstract: Universal machine-learned interatomic potentials (uMLIPs) are ranked by their formation-energy error against a DFT database, as if that database were ground truth, but independent DFT databases disagree. We benchmark fourteen uMLIPs on 1.45 million ordered intermetallics against AFLOW, which none trained on, and compare every energy and stability error with that disagreement. For half the structures, AFLOW, Alexandria, and OQMD differ by less than 6-8 meV/atom, but a minority differ by far more, raising the average to 16-30. That disagreement sets a floor on any model's measurable error. Re-referenced, the best uMLIP agrees with AFLOW as closely as Alexandria does, and the next four sit 2-7 meV/atom above the floor. Once broken elemental references are screened, the energy and stability rankings agree almost everywhere. Three apparent model failures shrink or vanish under the right control: poorly correlated shear moduli, thousands of "bad" DFT references, and the leader's inflated lead.

</details>


### [95] [From phase analysis to phase tracking: a robust approach to atomic-scale strain mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30665)
*Ying Han,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的原子尺度应变测量方法——几何相位跟踪分析(GPTA)，通过让傅里叶孔径实时跟踪每个像素的布拉格反射，解决了传统几何相位分析(GPA)在大应变、大旋转或错向晶粒下失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何相位分析(GPA)是原子分辨率应变测量的标准工具，但当布拉格反射在大应变或大旋转下移出固定傅里叶孔径、或在错向晶粒中，GPA就会失效。因此需要一种能跟踪移动布拉格反射的新方法，以扩展原子尺度应变计量到更苛刻的成像条件与束敏材料。

Method: 提出几何相位跟踪分析(GPTA)，其核心是让傅里叶孔径在实空间中逐像素跟踪布拉格反射的位置。窄孔径因此既能抑制噪声，又不会丢失已位移的反射。在13,795张不同成像条件下的模拟与实验原子分辨率图像上进行系统验证。

Result: GPTA将GPA的失效率从27%降至11.5%，精度最高提升335倍；并能给出跨晶粒的应变与位错图、对动力学散射消光的抵抗力以及置信度图，这些都是GPA所不具备的能力。

Conclusion: 跟踪孔径将倒空间剂量效率与实空间空间分辨率相结合，把原子尺度应变计量扩展到束敏感材料及具有挑战性的成像条件，显著优于传统GPA。

Abstract: Atomic-scale strain governs the electronic, ferroic and mechanical properties of materials, and atomic-resolution microscopy measures it most directly. Geometric phase analysis (GPA) is the standard tool, but it fails when a Bragg reflection moves outside its fixed Fourier aperture under large strain or rotation, or in misoriented grains. Here we introduce geometric phase tracking analysis (GPTA), whose Fourier aperture tracks the Bragg reflection at each pixel in real space. A narrow aperture can therefore reject noise without losing a displaced reflection. On 13,795 simulated and experimental atomic-resolution images under various imaging conditions, GPTA reduces GPA's failure rate from 27% to 11.5% with up to 335 times better accuracy. It also gives strain and dislocation maps across grains, resistance to dynamical-scattering extinction, and a confidence map, capabilities that GPA lacks. Tracking the aperture combines reciprocal-space dose efficiency with real-space spatial resolution and extends atomic-scale strain metrology to beam-sensitive materials and challenging imaging conditions.

</details>


### [96] [Universal Synthesis of Large-Area Hybrid Perovskite Monolayers Enabling Lithography-Free, Low-Static-Power Optoelectronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30764)
*Shankeerthan Kalyanasundaram,Swarit Dwivedi,Sanje Mahasivam,Christopher R. McNeill,Sudha Mokkapati*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种位阻工程三元溶剂生长策略，用于合成大横向尺寸的单层及少层 A2MX4 杂化钙钛矿，解决了此前单层杂化有机-无机钙钛矿因面外生长过快、成核失控和层间聚集而只能获得数微米薄片的问题。该策略以 (PEA)2CuCl4 为模型体系实现了横向面积超过 1000 μm² 的单层，具有广泛的材料普适性，并在此基础上制备出超低静态功耗、高光暗电流比的平面光电探测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层杂化有机-无机钙钛矿的溶液相合成受限于快速的平面外晶体生长、不可控成核及强烈的层间聚集，导致所得薄片横向尺寸往往仅有数微米，难以满足大面积器件与电极制备需求。因此需要一种能抑制面外堆叠、实现大横向面积单层/少层钙钛矿的可扩展合成方法。

Method: 提出一种"位阻工程三元溶剂生长策略"：将醇类共溶剂与胺类添加剂相结合，以抑制平面外堆叠；利用分子动力学与量子化学计算揭示机理（长链醇重组前驱体溶剂化、溶剂挥发性延长退火过程中的边缘介导前驱体输运）。以 (PEA)2CuCl4 为模型体系，并通过改变有机间隔阳离子（PEA、F-PEA、PentA）、金属中心（Cu、Mn、Pb）和卤素（Cl、Br、I）验证策略普适性。此外，利用大横向尺寸实现物理荫罩直接无溶剂电极接触制备，避免了传统光刻溶剂造成的化学降解。

Result: 在 (PEA)2CuCl4 体系中获得了横向面积超过 1000 μm² 的单层薄片，较传统方法实现了极大的尺寸提升；方法对多种有机间隔阳离子、金属中心和卤素组合均具普适性。基于孤立超薄 (PEA)2PbI4 沟道的平面器件表现出低至 10^-15 ~ 10^-13 A 量级的超低暗电流，实现飞瓦至皮瓦级静态功耗（5 V 下约 0.2 pW），相比滴涂薄膜降低 250 倍，同时保持 4.3 × 10² 的高光暗电流比。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了精确的位阻与溶剂化控制范式，实现了组分多样、可扩展、低静态功耗的单层二维钙钛矿光电器件，为单层杂化钙钛矿的大面积溶液相合成及低功耗光电子应用开辟了道路。

Abstract: Solution-phase synthesis of monolayer hybrid organic-inorganic perovskites is constrained by rapid out-of-plane crystal growth, uncontrolled nucleation, and strong interlayer aggregation, yielding flakes of only a few μm. We introduce a steric-engineering ternary-solvent growth strategy to synthesize monolayer and few-layer A2MX4 hybrid perovskites with exceptionally large surface areas. By combining alcohol cosolvents with an amine additive, out-of-plane stacking is suppressed. Using (PEA)2CuCl4 as a model system, monolayers with lateral areas exceeding 1000 μm2 are achieved, representing a massive dimensional increase over conventional methods. Molecular dynamics and quantum-chemical calculations show that longer-chain alcohols reorganize precursor solvation, while solvent volatility prolongs edge-mediated precursor transport during annealing. This strategy demonstrates universal applicability across diverse organic spacer cations (PEA+, F-PEA+, PentA+), metal centers (Cu2+, Mn2+, Pb2+), and halides (Cl-, Br-, I-). The large lateral dimensions enable direct, solvent-free electrical contact fabrication via physical shadow masking, bypassing chemical degradation from solvents used in conventional lithography. Planar devices fabricated from isolated ultrathin (PEA)2PbI4 channels exhibit ultra-low dark currents down to the 10^-15 - 10^-13 A range, enabling femtowatt-to-picowatt static power consumption, ~ 0.2 pW at 5 V, a 250-fold reduction relative to drop-cast films, while maintaining a high light-to-dark current ratio of 4.3 x 102. This work establishes precise steric and solvation control as a paradigm for scalable, compositionally diverse, low-static-power monolayer 2D perovskite optoelectronics.

</details>


### [97] [Designing sustainable high-entropy alloys directly from mixed oxides: A thermodynamic perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30870)
*Albert Linda,Shiv Shankar,Dierk Raabe*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文开发了基于CALPHAD的热力学框架，用于确定CoCrFeMnNi系混合氧化物前驱体在氢基直接还原（HyDR）中实现完全金属化所需的成分-温度-H₂/H₂O窗口，并揭示了各元素对氧化物稳定性影响的可变性及还原路径复杂性与残余氧化物稳定性之间的非相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢基直接还原混合氧化物前驱体是生产可持续合金的替代路线，可绕过传统冶金中提取、精炼、重熔等高碳排步骤。但由于金属固溶体和氧化物固溶体的形成会改变还原金属相与未还原氧化物的化学活度，使各相平衡相互耦合，无法从纯单一氧化物的稳定性推断，因此多组分氧化物完全金属化的热力学极限和最优H₂/H₂O比例尚未确立。

Method: 开发一种基于CALPHAD的热力学框架，建立成分-温度-H₂/H₂O窗口，用于分析CoCrFeMnNi基混合氧化物的完全金属化条件，考察各元素对氧化物稳定性的影响、元素效应的成分依赖性以及还原路径复杂性与残余氧化物稳定性的关系。

Result: Mn和Cr普遍提高氧化物稳定性，需要更高的H₂/H₂O比例才能还原；而Co、Fe、Ni降低氧化物稳定性，可在较低H₂/H₂O比下还原。但每种元素的影响并非固定，取决于起始混合氧化物的成分，且当最后还原的氧化物发生变化时其影响可能反转。此外，还原路径的复杂度与去除最终氧的难度不相关：还原步骤少的体系仍可能终止于高度稳定的残余氧化物，而大量部分还原的体系其最终残余氧化物的稳定性可能基本不变。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将残余氧化物稳定性与H₂/H₂O比例关联，确定了热力学极限，为直接从混合氧化物前驱体设计可持续多组分合金提供了指导原则。

Abstract: Hydrogen-based direct reduction (HyDR) of mixed oxide precursors offers an alternative route to produce sustainable alloys, bypassing the sequential extraction, refining, and remelting steps that dominate the carbon footprint of conventional metallurgy and individual metal production. However, the thermodynamic limits and most effective H$_2$/H$_2$O ratio needed for full metallization of multicomponent oxides are not yet established. This is because the formation of metallic and oxide solid solutions alters the chemical activity of both the reduced metallic phases and the unreduced oxides, coupling their equilibria in ways that cannot be deduced from the stability of the pure individual oxides. Herein, we develop a CALPHAD-based thermodynamic framework to establish the compoistion-temperature-H$_2$/H$_2$O window for complete metallization of CoCrFeMnNi-based oxide mixtures. Mn and Cr generally increase oxide stability, while Co, Fe, and Ni decrease it, allowing reduction at lower H$_2$/H$_2$O ratios. The effect of each element is not fixed; it depends on the starting mixed oxide composition and can reverse when the last oxide to reduce changes. Furthermore, we show that the reduction pathway complexity does not correlate with the difficulty of removing the final oxygen: a system with few intermediate transformation steps can still terminate as a highly stable residual oxide, whereas extensive partial reduction may leave the stability of the final residual oxide essentially unchanged. By linking residual-oxide stability to the H$_2$/H$_2$O ratio, the framework identifies the thermodynamic limits and provides guidelines for designing sustainable multicomponent alloys directly from mixed oxide precursors.

</details>


### [98] [Thermal expansion mechanisms, heat transport, and band-gap renormalization in strongly anharmonic Rb$_2$SO$_4$, Rb$_2$Ca$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$, and Ba$_3$Lu(BO$_3$)$_3$ from machine-learned interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30918)
*Ekaterina Spirande,Rustam Arabov,Sofya Demina,Nikita Rybin,Alexander Shapeev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用主动学习矩张量势的分子动力学，研究了三种结构复杂材料（Rb$_2$SO$_4$、Rb$_2$Ca$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$、Ba$_3$Lu(BO$_3$)$_3$）的晶格动力学对其热学和电子性质的影响，验证了衍射所提出的热膨胀机制，并指出非谐性度量不足以单独决定热输运，同时计算了声子诱导的带隙重整化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这三种材料结构复杂，其高温X射线衍射所揭示的热膨胀此前被解释为特定的多面体重排机制，但缺乏原子层面的晶格动力学支撑；同时需要厘清非谐性与热输运、电子性质之间的关系。

Method: 采用基于密度泛函理论数据训练的主动学习矩张量势（moment tensor potentials）进行分子动力学模拟，研究晶格动力学；通过非谐性度量 σ^A（比较原子间作用力的非谐贡献与总力之比）量化非谐性；用 Green-Kubo 方法计算晶格热导率；并计算含晶格热膨胀的声子诱导带隙重整化（包括零点重整化）。

Result: σ^A 在 β-Rb₂SO₄、Rb₂Ca₂(SO₄)₃、Ba₃Lu(BO₃)₃ 中约 0.3–0.5，在取向无序的 α-Rb₂SO₄ 中约 0.75；Rb₂Ca₂(SO₄)₃ 和 α-Rb₂SO₄ 的虚频简谐模在有限温度下被稳定化。模拟复现了实测热膨胀，并支持衍射提出的机制：β-Rb₂SO₄ 沿 c 轴膨胀源于波纹状 Rb 链的伸长与拉直，BO₃ 重取向伴随 Ba₃Lu(BO₃)₃ 的各向异性膨胀，多面体连接的弯曲限制了弱膨胀方向和立方 Rb₂Ca₂(SO₄)₃ 中的膨胀。σ^A 相近的化合物间 Green-Kubo 晶格热导率相差超过一个数量级。带隙重整化在 1250 K 时 Rb₂Ca₂(SO₄)₃ 达 2.7 eV、Ba₃Lu(BO₃)₃ 达 1.7 eV，650 K 时 β-Rb₂SO₄ 达 1.4 eV；三者的零点重整化分别为 0.64、0.70、0.31 eV。

Conclusion: 温度依赖的分子动力学模拟能够复现并验证结构复杂材料的热膨胀微观机制；非谐性度量 σ^A 虽能量化非谐性强弱，但不足以单独决定热输运，晶格热导率还受其他因素影响；声子诱导的带隙重整化（含热膨胀）在这些材料中可高达数 eV，其中零点重整化贡献显著。

Abstract: Rb$_2$SO$_4$, Rb$_2$Ca$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$, and Ba$_3$Lu(BO$_3$)$_3$ are structurally complex materials whose thermal expansion has been interpreted, on the basis of high-temperature x-ray diffraction, in terms of specific polyhedral rearrangements. We study their lattice dynamics and its consequences for thermal and electronic properties using molecular dynamics with actively learned moment tensor potentials trained on density-functional theory data. The anharmonicity measure $σ^A$ (compares the anharmonic contribution to the interatomic forces with the total forces acting on atoms) reaches $\approx$0.3-0.5 for $β$-Rb$_2$SO$_4$, Rb$_2$Ca$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$, and Ba$_3$Lu(BO$_3$)$_3$ and $\approx$0.75 for orientationally disordered $α$-Rb$_2$SO$_4$, and the imaginary harmonic modes of Rb$_2$Ca$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$ and $α$-Rb$_2$SO$_4$ are stabilized at finite temperature. The simulations reproduce the measured thermal expansion and support the mechanisms proposed from diffraction: expansion along $c$ in $β$-Rb$_2$SO$_4$ arises from both elongation and straightening of corrugated Rb chains, BO$_3$ reorientation accompanies the anisotropic expansion of Ba$_3$Lu(BO$_3$)$_3$, and bending of polyhedral linkages limits expansion along the weakly expanding directions and in cubic Rb$_2$Ca$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$. Green-Kubo lattice thermal conductivities differ by more than an order of magnitude between compounds with comparable $σ^A$, showing that the anharmonicity measure alone does not determine heat transport. Phonon-induced band-gap renormalization, including lattice thermal expansion, reaches 2.7 and 1.7 eV at 1250 K in Rb$_2$Ca$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$ and Ba$_3$Lu(BO$_3$)$_3$ and 1.4 eV at 650 K in $β$-Rb$_2$SO$_4$, with zero-point renormalizations of 0.64, 0.70, and 0.31 eV for $β$-Rb$_2$SO$_4$, Rb$_2$Ca$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$, and Ba$_3$Lu(BO$_3$)$_3$, respectively.

</details>


### [99] [Piezo-Kerr effect in altermagnets and noncollinear antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30929)
*Yuqi Liu,Qiu-Shi Huang,Xinfeng Chen,Yilin Han,Run-Wu Zhang,Zhi-Ming Yu,Yugui Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出利用对称性（而非仅将其视为约束）来设计控制场，通过表征匹配原理将控制场与响应张量耦合，发现磁光克尔效应中存在"压电-克尔效应"：在原本克尔响应被禁止的122个磁点群中，有16个群允许应变线性诱导克尔响应，并通过第一性原理计算在MnTe₂、CoF₂及Cr₂Se₂O、Cr₂Te₂O单层中验证了可开关、可反转的大克尔旋转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性通常被看作对物理响应的约束，但作者希望将其转化为设计控制场的工具，从而在补偿磁体等对称性禁止态中实现大的、可开关的克尔响应，克服传统方法需要净磁化或复杂调控的局限。

Method: 采用表征匹配原则：当控制场的某分量（或其组合）与目标响应分量在系统磁点群（MPG）下属于同一不可约表示时，线性耦合被对称性允许。将该原则应用于磁光克尔效应，得到压电-克尔效应，并系统枚举全部122个磁点群，找出16个"未应变时克尔响应禁止、但线性应变诱导贡献允许"的群。随后用第一性原理计算验证块体MnTe₂、CoF₂以及Cr₂Se₂O、Cr₂Te₂O单层中的应变激活克尔旋转及其符号反转。

Result: 在16个磁点群中，未应变状态克尔响应被禁止，而特定线性应变可激活克尔响应；这些群由具有对称性强制零净磁化的交错磁体（altermagnet）和非共线反铁磁体实现。第一性原理计算表明，无应变时克尔旋转为零，0.5–3%应变可产生数十至数百毫度的旋转，且反转匹配的应变通道会反转克尔符号。

Conclusion: 本文确立了一条在补偿磁体中从对称性禁止态产生大且可开关克尔响应的通用途径，证明对称性可作为设计工具，通过应变线性耦合激活并反转原本被禁止的磁光响应。

Abstract: Symmetry is usually viewed as a constraint on physical responses, but it can also be used to design the fields that control them. Here, we adopt the representation-matching principle used to identify conjugate fields of order parameters and apply it to response tensors. A linear coupling, the key ingredient for sign-switching control, is symmetry-allowed when a component (or a combination of components) of the control field and the selected response component share the same irreducible representation of the magnetic point group (MPG) of the system. Applied to the magneto-optical Kerr effect, this principle yields a piezo-Kerr effect, in which specific strain combinations activate a Kerr response forbidden in the unstrained state. Enumerating all 122 MPGs, we identify 16 groups in which the unstrained Kerr response is forbidden while a linear strain-induced contribution is allowed. These groups are realized by altermagnets and noncollinear antiferromagnets with symmetry-enforced vanishing net magnetization. The active strain channels and the associated first-order sign reversals follow directly from symmetry. First-principles calculations for bulk MnTe$_{2}$ and CoF$_{2}$, and Cr$_{2}$Se$_{2}$O and Cr$_{2}$Te$_{2}$O monolayers confirm these predictions. In these materials, the Kerr rotation vanishes without strain, whereas 0.5-3% strain produces rotations ranging from tens to hundreds of millidegrees, and reversing the matched strain channel reverses the Kerr sign. These results establish a general route to large and switchable Kerr responses emerging from symmetry-forbidden states in compensated magnets.

</details>


### [100] [Chiroptical Valleytronic in SnSe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30930)
*Yiming Pan,Sotirios Fragkos,Umberto Dellasette,Quentin Courtade,Dominique Descamps,Stéphane Petit,Fabio Caruso,Samuel Beaulieu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了SnSe中由量子几何决定的手性光选择定则，其产生依赖螺旋度的动量空间载流子布居不平衡（谷选择激发），并展示了由此引发的亚皮秒谷极化衰减与可逆瞬态光电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bloch电子的量子几何性质在调控固体中的光-物质相互作用和输运现象中起核心作用，但量子几何如何具体支配谷选择性的光-物质相互作用、谷极化及光电流产生，仍缺乏微观层面的理解，尤其是在具有隐藏和瞬态对称性破缺的范德华材料中。

Method: 结合时间分辨与角分辨极紫外光电子能谱（tr-ARPES）实验，以及光激发载流子动力学的从头算实时模拟；通过晶体对称性对量子几何张量的约束，理论推导谷圆二色性的幅度；并模拟电子-声子耦合介导的谷间散射及瞬态光电流。

Result: 在SnSe中观测到圆偏振光照射下时间反演对称性破缺导致的相反晶格动量处的谷选择性激发；理论证明谷圆二色性幅度受晶体对称性通过量子几何张量强烈约束；手性诱导的谷极化在电子-声子耦合介导的谷间散射下于亚皮秒时间尺度衰减；并理论证明相反动量谷间的布居不平衡可产生螺旋度可逆的瞬态光电流。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了量子几何如何支配范德华材料（具有隐藏和瞬态对称性破缺）中的谷选择性光-物质相互作用、谷极化与光电流产生，提供了微观层面的深刻认识。

Abstract: Quantum geometric properties of Bloch electrons play a central role in governing light-matter interactions and transport phenomena in solids. Here, we report quantum-geometric chiroptical selection rules that generate a helicity-dependent momentum-space population imbalance in the group-IV monochalcogenide SnSe. By combining time- and angle-resolved extreme-ultraviolet photoemission spectroscopy with ab initio real-time simulations of photoexcited carrier dynamics, we identify valley-selective excitation at opposite crystal momenta arising from the breaking of time-reversal symmetry under circularly polarized illumination. We theoretically demonstrate that the magnitude of valley circular dichroism is strongly constrained by crystal symmetry through its influence on the quantum geometric tensor, the key quantity underlying valley optical selection rules. The resulting chiroptically induced valley polarization decays on sub-picosecond timescales through intervalley scattering mediated by electron-phonon coupling. Furthermore, we show theoretically that the population imbalance between valleys at opposite momenta gives rise to a helicity-reversible transient photocurrent. These findings provide microscopic insight into how quantum geometry governs valley-selective light-matter interactions, valley polarization, and photocurrent generation in van der Waals materials exhibiting hidden and transient symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [101] [Intensity-driven Mott-like transition in a 2D Ruddlesden-Popper perovskite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30964)
*Koushik Mondal,Amit Haldar,Indrajit Mondal,Arpita Dutta,Himanshu Bhatt,Biswajit Mahata,Soumili Banerjee,Hirendra Nath Ghosh,Satyaprasad P Senanayak,Shovon Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维 Ruddlesden-Popper 型钙钛矿 (BA)₂PbI₄ 在红外飞秒激发下的非线性光学性质与超快载流子动力学，发现随泵浦强度增大，非线性吸收从饱和吸收（SA）转变为反饱和吸收（RSA），并伴随折射率变化，揭示了一种由强度驱动的 Mott 类相变：从激子主导区过渡到稠密电子-空穴等离子体区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维 Ruddlesden-Popper 型卤化物钙钛矿因强激子效应和载流子密度依赖的光学响应而成为有潜力的非线性光学材料，但其在红外激发下（与激子跃迁的双光子能量共振）的非线性吸收机制、载流子动力学及激子-等离子体相变行为尚需深入阐明，以推动其红外波段全光操作应用。

Method: 采用 1030 nm 飞秒泵浦的非线性 Z 扫描测量，系统考察不同泵浦强度下非线性吸收系数和折射率的演化；并利用相同激发条件下的强度依赖瞬态吸收光谱（TAS）追踪超快载流子动力学，结合对 Mott 密度的估算，判断激子相与电子-空穴等离子体相的转变。

Result: Z 扫描显示，随着泵浦强度增加，非线性吸收由饱和吸收（SA）切换为反饱和吸收（RSA），呈现明显的强度依赖演化；瞬态吸收光谱揭示在估算的 Mott 密度之上存在长寿命载流子群体，表明系统由激子主导区向稠密电子-空穴等离子体区发生清晰的 Mott 类相变。

Conclusion: (BA)₂PbI₄ 在红外脉冲激发下表现出高强度、可切换的非线性光学响应及 Mott 类相变，使其成为红外波段全光操作的理想候选材料。

Abstract: Two-dimensional Ruddlesden-Popper-type halide perovskites have emerged as promising cutting-edge nonlinear optical materials due to their strong excitonic effects and carrier-density-dependent optical responses. In this work, we investigate the nonlinear optical properties and ultrafast carrier dynamics of (BA)$_2$PbI$_4$ under infrared excitation, resonant with the two-photon energy of its excitonic transition. Using a nonlinear femtosecond Z-scan measurement with a 1030\,nm pump pulse, we observe a pronounced intensity-dependent evolution of the nonlinear absorption coefficient and the refractive index. With increasing pump intensity, a switch from saturable absorption to reverse saturable absorption emerges, suggesting an intensity-driven Mott-like transition. To elucidate the underlying carrier dynamics, intensity-dependent transient absorption spectroscopy was performed under the same excitation intensities. This reveals a long-lived charge carrier population above the estimated Mott density, reflecting a clear phase transition from an exciton-dominated regime to a dense electron-hole plasma regime. Moreover, such an intensity-driven, high, and switchable nonlinear optical response, along with a Mott-like transition under infrared pulse excitation, makes the 2D (BA)$_2$PbI$_4$ perovskites suitable for all-optical operation in the infrared regime.

</details>


### [102] [Donor-acceptor recombination in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30977)
*Konstantin Iakoubovskii,Andrey Katrusha,Weihua Peng,Jianguo Peng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了此前被归因于金刚石中施主-受主（D-A）复合的多个光学系统（2.8 eV 和 2.3 eV 宽带、2.0–2.7 eV 与 4.0–5.0 eV 的锐线系列），提出这些现象更宜用缺陷中心的内部跃迁来解释，并针对 2.3 eV 频带提出了更符合实际的深能级复合模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究将金刚石中若干光学系统归因于施主-受主（D-A）复合，但该模型在低杂质浓度样品中存在物理上的不合理之处（如远程施主与受主间的隧穿概率极低），因此需要重新检验并给出更一致的解释。

Method: 作者系统性地重新分析了多个光学系统（2.8 eV 和 2.3 eV 宽带、2.0–2.7 eV 及 4.0–5.0 eV 锐线系列）的实验观测，将 D-A 复合模型与缺陷中心内部跃迁模型进行对比论证；针对 2.3 eV 频带及其伴随的磷光和热释光现象，提出了电子和空穴分别被氮、硼缺陷俘获后于深能级中心复合的模型。

Result: 在所有考察的系统中，一个或多个缺陷中心的内部跃迁比 D-A 模型更能一致地解释实验现象。2.3 eV 频带的情况较边缘化，反对 D-A 复合的关键论据是在杂质浓度极低（约 0.1 ppm）的金刚石中，远程施主与受主之间隧穿概率低得几乎不可能。更现实的模型为：电子和空穴分别被氮和硼缺陷俘获，随后在一个深能级中心复合，该中心被暂定为最近的相邻硼-氮（B-N）对。

Conclusion: 金刚石中这些光学系统不宜归因于 D-A 复合，缺陷内部跃迁（特别是涉及 B-N 对深能级的俘获-复合过程）提供了更一致的解释。

Abstract: We re-examine several optical systems that have previously been attributed to donor-acceptor (D-A) recombination in diamond, including the broad 2.8 eV and 2.3 eV bands, and the sharp-lined series in the ranges 2.0-2.7 eV and 4.0-5.0 eV. In all cases, internal transitions at one or multiple defect centers provide a more consistent explanation of the experimental observations than the D-A model. The 2.3 eV case is marginal, where the key argument against the D-A recombination is the improbably low probability of tunneling between remote donors and acceptors in diamonds with very low concentrations of impurities (~0.1 ppm). A more realistic model for the 2.3 eV band and the associated phosphorescence and thermoluminescence phenomena is trapping of electrons and holes at nitrogen and boron defects, respectively, followed by recombination at a deep center, which is tentatively assigned to the nearest neighbor B-N pair.

</details>


### [103] [Role of Chalcogen-p States in the Optical Response of Bulk PtSe2 and PtTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30978)
*Ji Eun Lee,R. Mathew Roy,Premakumar Yanda,Chandra Shekhar,Claudia Felser,Martin Dressel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过红外光谱与DFT计算对比，揭示PtSe₂与PtTe₂虽结构、价态相同，但因硫族p带能级位置差异，导致Drude谱重与中红外吸收的显著不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PtSe₂和PtTe₂是同结构、同价态的层状type-II狄拉克半金属，却表现出迥异的电子性质，需探究其光学响应差异的微观起源。

Method: 在10–300 K范围内测量体晶的面内红外反射率，将带间跃迁对光电导的贡献与分辨能带的密度泛函理论计算进行对比。

Result: 低温下PtTe₂的Drude谱重比PtSe₂大一个数量级；PtTe₂出现宽的中红外吸收连续谱，而PtSe₂吸收很弱。计算将其归因于硫族p带到Pt-d带的跃迁。PtTe₂中硫族p带能级更高，降低了跃迁起始能量并增大半金属带重叠，与更大的Drude谱重一致。type-II狄拉克点远低于费米面，故形成狄拉克锥的能带间跃迁对带间电导贡献很小。

Conclusion: PtSe₂与PtTe₂的光学及电输运差异源于硫族p带相对能级的不同（PtTe₂中p带更高），而非type-II狄拉克点附近的跃迁；狄拉克锥远离费米面，对带间电导无显著贡献。

Abstract: The type-II Dirac semimetals PtSe$_2$ and PtTe$_2$ are isostructural and isovalent layered compounds but exhibit distinct electronic properties. We measure the in-plane infrared reflectivity of bulk crystals between 10 and 300 K and compare the interband contribution to the optical conductivity with band-resolved density-functional calculations. At low temperature, the Drude spectral weight of PtTe$_2$ is an order of magnitude larger than that of PtSe$_2$. PtTe$_2$ also shows a broad mid-infrared absorption continuum, whereas the absorption of PtSe$_2$ remains weak. The calculations attribute this continuum to transitions from a predominantly chalcogen-$p$ band into a Pt-$d$ band. In PtTe$_2$, the chalcogen-$p$ band lies higher in energy, which lowers the onset of these transitions and increases the semimetallic band overlap, in line with the larger Drude spectral weight. The type-II Dirac points lie far below the Fermi level, so that transitions between the bands forming the Dirac cones do not contribute appreciably to the interband conductivity.

</details>


### [104] [Nitrogen vacancy cluster-mediated ferroelectric domain growth in wurtzite AlN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31084)
*Zijian Zhou,Kan-Hao Xue,Ziqi Pang,Heng Yu,Shanzhong Xie,Hang Liu,Haiming Qin,Yi Tong,Bowen Su,Yi Yang,Tian-Ling Ren,Xiangshui Miao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文结合DFT、深度势能分子动力学与实验表征，阐明氮空位（V_N）如何调控AlN基铁电薄膜的反转畴生长与矫顽场E_c：理想晶体沿极轴择优生长，V_N局域削弱晶格耦合并促进成核，其空间分布与时间序列共同决定畴形貌，且Sc掺杂促进V_N形成从而降低E_c。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AlN基铁电材料虽有高剩余极化、热稳定、弱尺寸效应、宽带隙及后道工艺兼容等优点，但其高矫顽场E_c和极化反转机制不明确（理论预测沿极轴择优畴生长，而实验显示近各向同性或垂直极轴择优生长）限制了其在高密度非易失存储中的应用。

Method: 结合密度泛函理论（DFT）计算、深度势能分子动力学（Deep Potential molecular dynamics）模拟与实验表征，系统研究氮空位V_N对畴生长方向、形貌及E_c的调控机制，并考察Sc掺杂对V_N形成与缺陷介导成核的影响。

Result: 理想晶体中沿极轴的强晶格耦合驱动畴沿该方向择优生长；V_N局域削弱该耦合并促进极化成核；V_N的空间分布及其诱导反转的时间序列共同决定反向畴的生长方向与形貌；Sc掺杂促进V_N形成，从而增强缺陷介导成核并降低E_c。

Conclusion: 建立了跨尺度机制，统一解释薄膜畴生长动力学与宏观E_c，调和了实验观测与理想晶体模拟的矛盾，并为低电压、可靠、可扩展的铁电器件提供了缺陷工程原则。

Abstract: Aluminum nitride-based ferroelectrics combine high remanent polarization, thermal stability, weak size effects, a wide bandgap and back-end process compatibility, making them promising for high density non-volatile memory. However, their high coercive field ($E_\mathrm{c}$) and unclear polarization switching mechanism limit further applications. Theoretical predictions indicate preferential reversed domain growth along the polar axis, whereas experiments show nearly isotropic growth or preferential growth perpendicular to this axis. Here, we combine density functional theory, Deep Potential molecular dynamics and experimental characterization to determine how nitrogen vacancies ($V_\mathrm{N}$) regulate domain growth and $E_\mathrm{c}$. In ideal crystal, strong lattice coupling along the polar axis drives preferential domain growth in this direction. $V_\mathrm{N}$ locally weakens this coupling and promotes polarization nucleation. The spatial distribution of $V_\mathrm{N}$ and the temporal sequence of vacancy induced switching jointly determine the direction and morphology of reversed domain growth. We further show that scandium doping promotes $V_\mathrm{N}$ formation, thereby enhancing defect mediated nucleation and reducing $E_\mathrm{c}$. These findings establish a multiscale mechanism governing thin-film domain-growth kinetics and macroscopic $E_\mathrm{c}$, reconciling experimental observations with ideal crystal simulations and providing defect engineering principles for low-voltage, reliable and scalable ferroelectric devices.

</details>


### [105] [Tracking the Emergence and Persistence of Abnormal Grain Growth in an Aluminum Alloy using Diffraction-Contrast Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31176)
*Helmuth-André Schulz-Harder,Karolína Gutbrod,Madlen Atzen,Markus Ziehmer,Thomas Wilhelm,Orkun Furat,Yubin Zhang,Jette Oddershede,Haixing Fang,Pierre-Olivier Autran,Wolfgang Ludwig,Marc Bernacki,Volker Schmidt,Carl E. Krill,Jules M. Dake*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合同步辐射衍射衬度断层扫描与相衬断层扫描，对铝合金 AA5252 进行三维晶粒与第二相颗粒追踪，发现异常晶粒长大的出现与持续主要由相邻晶粒尺寸和高角晶界决定，而非局部颗粒密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常晶粒长大本质上是三维过程，需要三维追踪才能理解其机制；同时需厘清第二相颗粒分布、晶界特征与相邻晶粒尺寸对异常长大出现及持续的影响。

Method: 采用同步辐射衍射衬度断层扫描（DCT）与相衬断层扫描（PCT）表征 AA5252 铝合金的三维晶粒结构，并定量第二相颗粒的尺寸与空间分布；通过中断退火实验追踪异常晶粒的持续长大，以及一颗原本属于基体的晶粒向异常长大的转变。

Result: 第二相颗粒有效抑制了基体晶粒长大，但局部颗粒密度的差异与异常晶粒的出现及后续快速长大无明显相关性；晶体学取向差尤其是相邻晶粒尺寸是异常长大速率的更强调控因素。基体晶粒在被吞并时往往尺寸较小且与异常晶粒构成高角晶界。那颗由正常转为异常长大的晶粒，在其转变前就已完全被较小的高角晶界基体晶粒包围，且局部颗粒密度略高于平均值。

Conclusion: 相同的微结构特征（较小相邻晶粒尺寸与高角晶界）同时促进异常晶粒长大的出现与持续，但触发由正常长大转为快速长大的机制仍未明确。

Abstract: Abnormal grain growth is inherently three-dimensional, making 3D tracking of grain evolution essential for understanding its underlying mechanisms. This study combines synchrotron-based diffraction-contrast tomography and phase-contrast tomography to characterize the three-dimensional grain structure and quantify the size and spatial distribution of second-phase particles in the aluminum alloy AA5252. Using an interrupted annealing protocol, we tracked the persistent growth of abnormal grains and the emergence of abnormal growth in a grain initially belonging to the matrix. Although second-phase particles effectively suppressed grain growth in the matrix, variations in local particle density showed no apparent correlation with either the emergence or subsequent rapid growth of abnormal grains. Instead, crystallographic misorientation and, more notably, neighboring grain size were identified as stronger predictors of abnormal growth rate. Matrix grains were consumed more rapidly when they were relatively small and shared high-angle grain boundaries with the growing abnormal grain. The same trends were observed for the grain that underwent a transition from normal to abnormal growth. Remarkably, even before the onset of abnormal growth, this grain was surrounded by smaller matrix grains with exclusively high-angle grain boundaries, while the local particle density was slightly higher than average. These findings suggest that the same microstructural characteristics facilitate both the emergence and persistence of abnormal grain growth, although the mechanism triggering the transition to rapid growth remains unresolved.

</details>


### [106] [Anomalous polar chains in halide perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31184)
*Erik Fransson,Apinya Ngoipala,Priyanka Deswal,Ethan Berger,Nicholas C. Bristowe,Paul Erhart,Julia Wiktor*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在铁电卤化物钙钛矿CsGeBr3的立方相中，Ge偏离中心形成一维极性链，其弛豫时间尺度为纳秒级，比BaTiO3/PbTiO3慢四个数量级，且链逐条翻转，构成一维开关机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上对铁电钙钛矿中局域极性畸变的研究多集中于三维集体有序与电场驱动反转，但对一维极性关联在卤化物钙钛矿中的表现及其超慢动力学的认识不足，亟需揭示局域极性如何以低维形式长程有序地存在与演化。

Method: 采用大规模分子动力学模拟，并使用基于第一性原理训练的原子间势（机器学习势）对CsGeBr3（并与CsSnBr3、BaTiO3、PbTiO3对比）进行模拟分析。

Result: 在名义上的立方相中，Ge偏离中心形成延伸至少数十纳米、以纳秒时间尺度弛豫的一维极性链，其弛豫比BaTiO3和PbTiO3慢四个数量级，CsSnBr3居中。该立方相是一种动态无序态，极性相反的链在整个晶体中翻转，一维局域极化与零净极化共存。铁电相中同样的链构成一维开关机制，极化逐链反转。

Conclusion: 简单的三维钙钛矿结构能够承载长程一维极性关联和极慢的动态行为，一维极性链同时是铁电相极化逐链反转的微观机制。

Abstract: Local polar distortions, their collective ordering, and their field-driven reversal have been studied extensively in ferroelectric perovskites for decades. Here we identify an unusual behavior in the ferroelectric halide perovskite CsGeBr3 using large-scale molecular dynamics with first-principles-trained interatomic potentials. In its nominally cubic phase the Ge off-centering forms one-dimensional polar chains that extend over at least tens of nanometers and relax on nanosecond timescales, four orders of magnitude slower than in BaTiO3 and PbTiO3, with CsSnBr3 in between. The cubic CsGeBr3 phase is a dynamically disordered state in which oppositely polarized chains flip throughout the crystal, and a long-lived one-dimensional local polarization coexists with zero net polarization. A simple three-dimensional perovskite thus hosts long-ranged one-dimensional polar correlations with remarkably slow dynamics. In the ferroelectric phase the same chains set a one-dimensional switching mechanism, in which the polarization reverses chain by chain.

</details>


### [107] [Mn-deficient Ni-Mn-Ga alloys: phase transitions, magnetic properties, and disappearance of martensitic transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31206)
*Valentina Moskvina,Jaroslav Čapek,Michal Rameš,Denys Musiienko,Petr Veřtát,Alexej Perevertov,Ladislav Straka,Oleg Heczko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用定向凝固形成的成分梯度，在同一生长与热处理条件下系统研究Ni-Mn-Ga磁性形状记忆合金的成分-性能关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定向凝固产生的成分梯度通常被认为是不利的，但作者指出这些梯度为在相同生长和热处理条件下系统研究成分与性能关系提供了高效平台，可避免逐样制备带来的工艺差异干扰。

Method: 通过光学区熔法生长一个缺Mn和两个近化学计量比的Ni-Mn-Ga寡晶，采用X射线荧光成分映射（XRF mapping）、光学显微术、X射线衍射、低场磁性测量和振动样品磁强计（VSM）进行表征。利用铸锭内轴向成分梯度（主要由Mn、Ga重新分布引起，Ni分布相对均匀）考察成分与相变及磁性间的关系。

Result: 马氏体与前马氏体相变温度总体随Mn含量增加而升高；Mn含量20.5–22.0 at.%（e/a < 7.40）的试样在10–400 K内未检测到两种相变，而Mn含量≥22.1 at.%（e/a ≥ 7.46）的试样均观察到两种相变，暗示可能存在电子浓度阈值。饱和磁化强度随Mn含量增加而升高，在25.8 at.%时10 K达96.9 A m²/kg、300 K达71.2 A m²/kg。生长后退火提高了相变温度并改变了低场磁响应。

Conclusion: 成分梯度寡晶是研究具有相同加工历史的Ni-Mn-Ga合金中成分、结构与磁性能关系的有效平台；相变可能受电子浓度阈值控制。

Abstract: Composition gradients formed during directional solidification are generally considered undesirable in Ni-Mn-Ga magnetic shape-memory alloys. However, these gradients offer a highly effective platform for systematically investigating composition-property relationships under identical growth and heat-treatment conditions. To exploit this approach, one Mn-deficient and two near-stoichiometric Ni-Mn-Ga oligocrystals were grown by optical zone remelting and characterized by X-ray fluorescence mapping, optical microscopy, X-ray diffraction, low-field magnetic measurements, and vibrating-sample magnetometry. The ingots contained elongated grains and axial compositional gradients, primarily associated with the redistribution of Mn and Ga, whereas Ni remained relatively uniformly distributed. The martensitic and premartensitic transformation temperatures generally increased with increasing Mn content. Neither transformation was detected between 10 and 400 K in specimens containing 20.5-22.0 at.% Mn, all of which had $e/a < 7.40$. In contrast, both transformations were observed in the investigated specimens containing at least 22.1 at.% ($e/a \geq 7.46$). This abrupt change may indicate a possible electron-concentration threshold for the occurrence of these transformations. Saturation magnetization increased with Mn content up to 25.8 at.%, reaching 96.9 A m$^2$/kg at 10 K and 71.2 A m$^2$/kg at 300 K. Post-growth annealing increased the transition temperatures and changed the low-field magnetic response. The results indicate that composition-gradient oligocrystals serve as an effective platform for investigating the relationships between composition, structure, and magnetic properties in Ni-Mn-Ga alloys with the same processing history.

</details>

### [108] [Vacancy-driven topological transition of a Chern insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30241)
*Peize Ding,Raquel Queiroz*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过研究束缚于空位的带隙内环态，对陈绝缘体中的强无序相变进行了微观描述，揭示了环态作为非微扰自由度在强无序摧毁陈相过程中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强无序如何摧毁陈绝缘体拓扑相，以及无序相变的微观机制，现有描述缺乏微观自由度层面的清晰图景。作者旨在利用空位束缚的环态这一非微扰自由度，构建陈数改变的微观描述。

Method: 利用格林函数方法，构造环态杂化的能量依赖哈密顿量；在公度周期空位格点中，零能空位核的狄拉克反转解释占据态陈数的变化；用自洽玻恩近似与数值无序平均处理弱位置无序；用转移矩阵与多重分形标度研究独立分布的空位；并用局域陈标记与流纹理在实空间追踪相变。

Result: 零能空位核的狄拉克反转对应全系统占据态陈数的改变；弱位置无序与独立分布空位下的标度分析刻画了相变行为；局域陈标记和电流纹理在实空间中追踪了陈相的消失过程。

Conclusion: 非微扰的环态是强无序摧毁陈相的微观自由度，通过这些环态构建的微观描述成功刻画了强无序相变的机制与陈数改变。

Abstract: We develop a microscopic description of the strong-disorder transition in a Chern insulator using the in-gap ring states bound to individual vacancies. Using Green's function formalism, we construct an energy-dependent Hamiltonian describing their hybridization. In a commensurate periodic vacancy lattice, a Dirac inversion of the zero-energy vacancy kernel accounts for the change in the occupied-state Chern number of the full system. We treat weak positional disorder using the self-consistent Born approximation and numerical disorder averaging, and study independently distributed vacancies using transfer-matrix and multifractal scaling. The local Chern marker and current textures track the transition in real space. Together, these results highlight the role that nonperturbative ring states have as microscopic degrees of freedom through which strong disorder destroys the Chern phase.

</details>


### [109] [Charge-monitored Luttinger liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30271)
*Victor Gurarie,Leo Radzihovsky*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究不守恒粒子数、与超导体邻近的相互作用费米 Luttinger 液体在弱连续粒子密度测量下的相图，发现测量诱导的面积律相与粒子隧穿诱导的体积律相之间的竞争，且相边界由 Luttinger 参数对测量率的普适依赖关系决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出常规高激发一维系统描述范围的、不具有粒子数守恒的 Luttinger 液体中，测量诱导相变与粒子隧穿之间的竞争机制，并寻找相边界的普适性刻画。

Method: 在玻色化方法适用范围内，考虑与超导体邻近的、无粒子数守恒的相互作用费米 Luttinger 液体，施加对粒子密度的弱连续测量，分析测量（倾向面积律相）与粒子进出隧穿（倾向体积律相）之间的竞争。

Result: 系统的体积律相始终呈现"电荷模糊"(charge fuzzy)特征；相边界由 Luttinger 参数对测量率的普适依赖关系给出，这与更一般的高激发一维系统（不再由 Luttinger 液体框架描述）中非普适的相边界形成对比。

Conclusion: 在无粒子数守恒的 Luttinger 液体中，测量诱导相变由测量与粒子隧穿竞争主导，相边界具有普适性，揭示了此类系统区别于一般高激发一维系统的独特物理。

Abstract: We discuss phases of interacting fermionic Luttinger liquids without particle number conservation, such as those in proximity to a superconductor, subjected to weak continuous measurements of their particle density, in the regime where the bosonization approach is applicable. We show that the phases of these systems are controlled by the competition between measurements, which favor the area-law phase and particle tunneling in and out of the Luttinger liquid, which favors the volume-law phase. The volume-law phase in this system is always charge fuzzy. We show that within this framework, the phase boundary between the two phases is given by a universal dependence of the Luttinger parameter on the measurement rate. This behavior can be contrasted with the non-universal phase boundaries in more generic highly excited one dimensional systems whose behavior is no longer captured by the Luttinger liquid framework.

</details>


### [110] [Non-adiabatic Optimisation via Localisation-Delocalisation Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30301)
*Ryan Smith,Sabrina Yue Wang,Filippo Maria Gambetta,Raul A. Santos*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为 FRIO 的数字化量子退火算法，利用穿越多体局域化（MBL）转变的连续反向量子退火，并借助朗道-齐纳交叉密度来加速，使退火时间随系统尺寸从多项式级降为常数级，数值上几乎总是优于预优化角度的 QAOA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多经典优化问题可映射为自旋玻璃基态问题，而自旋玻璃是多体局域化的范式模型。作者希望将量子统计力学中关于 MBL 的直觉用于指导构造新型量子优化算法，并解决连续退火时间过长、难以在近中期设备上实现的问题。

Method: 作者研究穿过 MBL 转变的连续反向量子退火，通过考察淬火过程中朗道-齐纳（Landau-Zener）交叉的密度来加速；用有效的 Fokker-Planck 方程解释成功概率的演化行为；将连续动力学 Trotter 化并保持步数随系统尺寸增长温和，构造出名为 FRIO 的量子电路算法，并给出确定含时哈密顿量参数的定量策略。

Result: 该解释表明所需退火时间随系统尺寸 N 从 O(poly(N)) 改善为 O(1)（对于能量密度非零的参考态）。Trotter 化后的电路步数随系统尺寸增加仍保持适度。在 SK 模型等自旋玻璃问题上，数值结果表明采用可通过热化动力学高效选取电路参数的 FRIO，几乎总是优于预优化角度的常规 QAOA。

Conclusion: 利用 MBL 转变的物理直觉可以构造快速、可数字化且适合近中期设备的量子优化算法 FRIO，其在求解自旋玻璃基态问题上优于传统 QAOA，为量子优化提供了新方向。

Abstract: The promise of efficiently solving classical optimisation problems on quantum computers is tantalizing. As many such problems can be mapped to approximating the ground state of spin-glasses, paradigmatic models for studying many-body localisation, it is natural to ask if some of the intuition coming from quantum statistical mechanics can help guide the construction of novel quantum optimisation algorithms. In this work, we discuss how continuous reverse quantum annealing crossing a many-body localisation transition can be accelerated by considering the density of Landau-Zener crossings as the system is quenched. We provide an interpretation of this behaviour in terms of an effective Fokker-Planck equation for the success probability of the algorithm. This interpretation suggests an improvement in the required annealing time as a function of the system size $N$, which goes from $\mathcal{O}({\rm poly}(N))$ to $\mathcal{O}(1)$ for reference states with non-vanishing energy density. We show that the continuous dynamics can be Trotterised with a number of steps that remains modest as system size increases, making it appealing for near-term devices. For the resulting circuit implementation, we provide a quantitative strategy for determining the parameters of the time-dependent Hamiltonian. We compare the performance of our digitised algorithm, which we call FRIO, against that of the QAOA for finding the ground state of spin-glass problems, in particular the SK model. The numerical results suggest that with suitably selected circuit parameters which can be efficiently found using thermalizing dynamics, FRIO almost always outperforms the conventional QAOA ansatz with pre-optimized angles.

</details>


### [111] [Symmetry-Preserving Local Representation for Short-Range Interaction in Complex Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30726)
*Hao Cheng,Tong Yang,Hao Wu,Haokai Hong,Zhongrui Wang,Shu Ping Lau,Wanyu Lin,Kay Chen Tan,Ming Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种保持对称性的局域表示框架，通过边界钝化严格保证拓扑连续性与平移不变性，用于高效且准确地建模高无序材料中的短程相互作用，并降低一个数量级的计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度无序材料（如高熵合金）中建模短程相互作用极具挑战，因为构型空间巨大，且截断的理论模型会破坏局域对称性，难以准确刻画局域化学环境与扩展周期边界之间的冲突。

Method: 提出一种保持对称性的局域表示框架，通过边界钝化严格施加拓扑连续性和平移不变性约束；将该框架应用于高熵合金，以自主揭示非线性配体效应，并直接捕捉覆盖度依赖的多体相互作用，而无需显式周期展开。

Result: 该框架成功解析了局域化学环境与扩展周期边界之间的矛盾，在准确获取短程相互作用的同时，将计算复杂度降低了一个数量级，并提供了物理透明的短程序解读范式。

Conclusion: 通过边界钝化保持对称性的局域表示框架，为高无序凝聚态体系中的短程有序提供了一种准确、高效且物理透明的建模新途径。

Abstract: Modeling short-range interactions in highly disordered materials remains challenging due to the immense configuration space and the disruption of local symmetry in truncated theoretical models. Here, we propose a symmetry-preserving local representation framework that strictly enforces topological continuity and translational invariance via boundary passivation. Applied to high-entropy alloys, this approach autonomously unravels the nonlinear ligand effects modulating local reactivity and accurately captures coverage-dependent many-body interactions without explicit periodic expansion. By resolving the conflict between localized chemical environments and extended periodic boundaries, this framework not only reduces computational complexity by an order of magnitude but also provides a physically transparent paradigm for deciphering short-range order in complex condensed matter systems.

</details>


### [112] [Localization Contrast As a Fundamental Limit of Radiative Quantum Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30741)
*Arsineh Apelian,Tanay Tak,Xuance Jiang,Tsung-Yin Tsai,James S. Speck,Vojtech Vlcek*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 无序可对价带与导带态产生不同程度的局域化（局域化对比），本文在随机InGaN合金中证明这种局域化对比强烈影响载流子复合通道的竞争：局域化对比降低辐射复合的带间光跃迁矩阵元，而原子尺度无序同时破坏平移对称性、改变态的动量和相位结构，产生额外低Q分量，使俄歇复合耦合相对纯GaN增强逾五个数量级，从而微观解释了辐射与俄歇复合的竞争趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序可使价带和导带态局域化到不同程度，但这种"局域化对比"是否存在可观测后果尚不清楚；作者旨在阐明局域化对比如何调控半导体中相互竞争的光学（辐射复合）与载流子-载流子（俄歇复合）相互作用。

Method: 以随机In_xGa_(1-x)N合金为模型，将In含量从x=13.5%提高到22%，使价带顶态逐步局域化而导带底态保持相对离域；通过对比局域化对带间光跃迁矩阵元的影响，并分析原子尺度无序导致的原胞平移对称性破缺及其对动量空间与相位结构的改变，量化俄歇-迈特纳复合耦合的增强。

Result: 局域化对比会减小辐射复合所依赖的带间光动量矩阵元；而在俄歇-迈特纳复合中，仅局域化不足以决定耦合强度，原子尺度无序破坏原胞平移对称性并改变态的动量空间与相位结构，产生额外低Q分量，使俄歇耦合相对纯GaN增强超过五个数量级。

Conclusion: 研究从微观层面解释了辐射复合与俄歇复合的竞争趋势，并确立了"局域化对比"作为无序调控半导体中光学和载流子-载流子相互作用的一种普适机制。

Abstract: Disorder can localize valence- and conduction-band states to different degrees, yet whether such localization contrast has observable consequences remains poorly understood. Here, we show that localization contrast strongly impacts competing carrier-recombination channels. We illustrate this behavior in random In$_x$Ga$_{1-x}$N alloys. Increasing the In content from $x=13.5\%$ to $x=22\%$ progressively localizes the valence-band maximum state while leaving the conduction-band minimum state comparatively delocalized. This contrasting localization decreases the interband optical momentum matrix element underlying radiative recombination. For Auger-Meitner recombination, however, localization alone does not determine the coupling; atomic-scale disorder also breaks primitive-cell translational symmetry and modifies the momentum-space and phase structure of the states. This generates additional low-$Q$ components and enhances the Auger coupling by more than five orders of magnitude relative to pristine GaN. Thus, we provide a microscopic explanation for the competing trends in radiative and Auger recombination. More broadly, our results establish localization contrast as a mechanism through which disorder can control optical and carrier-carrier interactions in semiconductors.

</details>
